Economic Trends

2026年11月 16日

1/5 
2026～2027年度日本経済見通し（2026年 11月） 
（2026年 7-9月期 GDP1次速報後改定） 
経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト  新家 義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

日本・国内総生産（ＧＤＰ）成長率予測値

2026年度予測 実質＋０．９％ （前回 ＋１．０％） 
 名目＋３．６％ （前回 ＋３．２％）

2027年度予測 実質＋１．２％ （前回 ＋１．２％） 
 名目＋３．４％ （前回 ＋３．０％）

※前回は 2026年９月８日時点の弊社予測値

○ 実質ＧＤＰ成長率の見通しは 2026年度が＋0.9％（９月時点予測：＋1.0％）、27年度が＋1.2％
（同＋1.2％）である。暦年では 26年が＋0.9％（同＋0.9％）、27年が＋1.0％（同＋1.0％）とな
る。26年７-９月期のＧＤＰ成長率が前回予測時点での想定を下回ったことを反映し、26年度の
成長率見通しを若干下方修正したが、先行きの景気見通しに大きな変更はない。26年度後半は物
価上昇率の再加速が景気の重荷となるものの、企業部門の底堅さが下支えとなり、緩やかな回復
基調は維持されると予想する。また、27年度にはコスト上昇圧力が徐々に和らぐことに加え、食
料品の消費税減税による押し上げもあり、景気の回復感が徐々に強まるとみている。

○ 今回の予測では、2027年４月から２年間、飲食料品にかかる消費税率を１％に引き下げ、あわせ
て中低所得の就業者を対象に年間 6000億円程度の就業者負担軽減支援金を支給することを前提と
している。家計の購買力増大に伴って、27年度の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は0.2％ポイント強押し上げ
られると想定している。また、27年度の消費者物価指数（総合）の前年比上昇率は▲1.4％ポイン
ト、消費者物価指数（生鮮除く総合）は▲1.2％ポイント程度押し下げられるとみられる。

○ 26年７-９月期の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は前期比年率▲0.1％と、４四半期ぶりのマイナス成長となっ
た。個人消費が減少したほか、住宅投資や公共投資も成長率を押し下げた。ただし、個人消費の
減少には、４-６月期にみられた自動車やエアコンなどの耐久財需要の反動や、８～９月の天候不

### Page 2

2/5 
順といった一時的な要因が大きく影響したとみられる。一方、設備投資は高い伸びとなり、企業
部門の底堅さが確認された。前期まで３四半期連続で比較的高い成長が続いていたことも踏まえ
れば、今回の小幅なマイナス成長を悲観視する必要はない。家計と企業の回復に濃淡はみられる
ものの、日本経済は均してみれば緩やかな回復基調にあると評価できる。

○ 26年10-12月期は前期比年率＋0.6％と、再びプラス成長に戻ると予想する。７-９月期に個人消
費を押し下げた耐久財需要の反動や天候不順といった一時的な要因が和らぐことに加え、名目賃
金の高い伸びや雇用の改善が消費を下支えするとみられる。設備投資についても、高水準の企業
収益や人手不足を背景とした省力化・デジタル関連の投資需要は根強く、増加基調を維持するだ
ろう。輸出も、海外景気の底堅さや世界的なＡＩ関連需要の拡大を背景に、緩やかな増加が期待
できる。個人消費は力強さを欠くものの、設備投資や輸出が下支えとなることで、景気の緩やか
な回復基調は維持されるだろう。

○ 26年度後半については、既往の資源高や原材料費、物流費などの上昇が時間差を伴って川下へ波
及することが、景気の下押し要因となる。食料品や日用品、耐久財など幅広い品目への価格転嫁
が進むことに加え、ガソリン補助金の段階的縮小も物価を押し上げる。こうした物価上昇率の再
加速によって、足元で続いている実質賃金の改善が一服し、家計の実質購買力が再び圧迫される
可能性がある。また、企業にとっても原材料費や物流費などの上昇が収益の重荷となるだろう。

○ もっとも、こうしたコスト上昇による下押しに対して、高水準の企業収益が一定の緩衝材となる
ことに加え、ＡＩ関連需要の拡大が半導体製造装置や電子部品などの輸出・生産、設備投資を押
し上げていることは景気の下支え要因となる。人手不足を背景とした省力化投資やデジタル関連
投資への需要も根強く、設備投資は増加基調を維持するとみられる。また、海外景気の底堅さも
輸出を支えるだろう。個人消費についても、物価高による下押し圧力が強まるものの、高い賃上
げや底堅い雇用環境が支えとなり、腰折れする可能性は低い。家計部門は力強さを欠く一方、企
業部門は堅調さを維持しており、日本経済は緩やかな回復基調を続けると予想する。

○ 27年度については、原油高や原材料費上昇による物価上昇圧力が徐々に減衰することで、景気の
回復感は次第に強まるとみている。26年秋の原油高の影響が時間差を伴って波及するため、年度
前半は物価上昇圧力がなお残るものの、年度後半にかけて価格転嫁の動きは徐々に落ち着くと予
想する。物価上昇率の鈍化によって家計の実質購買力が改善し、高い賃上げもあいまって、個人
消費の回復力が強まるだろう。食料品への消費税減税と就業者負担軽減支援金の支給も消費を押
し上げる要因となる。企業部門では、コスト上昇圧力の緩和によって収益環境が改善することも
支えとなり、省力化やデジタル・ＡＩ関連を中心とした設備投資が増加する見込みである。輸出
についても、海外景気の底堅さやＡＩ関連需要が引き続き下支え要因となる。物価面からの逆風
が和らぐことで、家計と企業の双方で内需の持ち直しが進み、27年度の景気回復の勢いは 26年度

### Page 3

3/5 
よりも強まると予想する。

○ 先行きのリスクとして注意が必要なのは、米国を中心としたＡＩ関連投資の持続性である。米中
間選挙後、州・地方政府によるデータセンター建設への規制強化が進めば、計画の延期や投資コ
ストの増加を通じて、ＡＩ関連投資の拡大ペースが鈍化する可能性がある。加えて、巨額投資の
収益性や資金調達の持続性に対する懸念も強まっている。これまでの米国景気はＡＩ投資の拡大
と株高に支えられてきた面が大きいため、仮にＡＩブームに変調が生じれば、その影響はハイテ
ク業界にとどまらず、米国経済、ひいては世界経済に波及する恐れがある。ＡＩ関連投資が大幅
に減速すれば、日本の半導体製造装置や電子部品などの輸出・生産、設備投資にも悪影響が及ぶ
だろう。もっとも、建設規制の強化とＡＩ需要そのものの減少は別問題である。ＡＩの活用拡大
に伴う計算需要は引き続き極めて強く、大手テクノロジー企業の投資意欲も旺盛である。既に着
工済みのデータセンターや契約済みの設備投資案件も多く、規制の影響を受けて投資先の地域を
変更する動きも考えられる。ＡＩ関連投資の拡大ペースは幾分鈍化する可能性があるものの、失
速するとは考えにくく、先行きも高水準の投資が続くと予想している。

○ 消費者物価指数（生鮮食品除く総合、ＣＰＩコア）は 26年度が＋2.3％（９月時点予測：＋
2.4％）、27年度が＋1.3％（同＋1.1％）と予想する。26年９月のＣＰＩコアは前年比＋2.1％と
９カ月ぶりの＋２％台となったが、年度後半にかけて一段と上昇率が高まる可能性が高い。食料
品価格は足元で鈍化傾向に歯止めがかかりつつあり、日用品でも値上げの動きが明確化してい
る。26年春の資源価格上昇や調達難の影響に加え、包装資材費や物流費などのコスト増が時間差
を伴って川下へ転嫁されることが物価を押し上げる。ＡＩ需要の拡大や円安の影響による耐久財
価格の上昇も続くとみられる。また、既往の燃料価格上昇は電気・ガス料金に波及するほか、ガ
ソリン補助金の段階的縮小も押し上げ要因となる。なお、冬場の電気・ガス料金補助は一定の押
し下げ要因となるが、前年比への影響は限定的とみられる。企業の価格転嫁姿勢が積極化してい
ることもあり、27年初にかけてＣＰＩコアは前年比＋３％を上回る可能性が高い。

○ 27年度については、26年秋の原油高の影響が時間差を伴って川下へ波及するため、年度前半は物
価上昇圧力が根強く残るとみられる。もっとも、原油高や原材料費上昇によるコストプッシュ圧
力は次第に弱まり、消費税要因を除いても、年度後半にかけて物価上昇率は緩やかに鈍化してい
くと予想する。食料品や日用品、耐久財などへの価格転嫁の動きも次第に落ち着き、物価面から
の家計への下押し圧力が和らぐことで、個人消費の持ち直しが後押しされるだろう。一方、高い
賃上げに伴う人件費上昇や人手不足、企業の積極的な価格設定姿勢は続くとみられ、サービス価
格を中心とした基調的な物価上昇圧力は維持されるとみている。なお、今回の予測では、27年４
月から食料品の消費税率を８％から１％に引き下げることを前提としており、これにより 27年度
のＣＰＩコア上昇率が約1.2％ポイント押し下げられると想定している。

### Page 4

4/5 
○ 金融政策について、日銀は 26年12月の金融政策決定会合で政策金利を1.25％から1.50％に引き
上げると予想する。日銀は 10月会合で、基調的な物価上昇率が２％目標と概ね整合的な水準に達
したとの判断を示し、金融政策運営の重点は「２％への到達」から「２％での定着」へと移っ
た。今後は基調的な物価上昇率を２％程度で安定させることが重要になるが、物価上振れリスク
が残るなか、現在の金融環境はなお緩和的であり、日銀は中立金利に向けて金融緩和の度合いを
縮小していくと考えられる。

○ その後も27年３月に1.75％、６月に2.00％へと、３カ月程度の間隔で追加利上げを行うと予想
する。27年度にはコスト要因による物価上昇圧力が徐々に弱まるとみられるものの、賃金上昇や
人手不足を背景とした基調的な物価上昇圧力は維持されるだろう。物価上振れへの警戒が残るな
か、日銀は段階的な利上げを継続すると予想する。なお、今回の利上げ局面の最終到達点（ター
ミナルレート）は2.0％と見込むが、中立金利の水準には大きな不確実性がある。基調的な物価上
昇率が想定以上に上振れれば 2.0％を超える利上げもあり得るだろう。一方、景気の下振れや既往
の利上げの累積的な影響が想定以上に強まる場合には、利上げペースが鈍化する可能性もある。

なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限

史新高。展望 4Q26，我們預期伺服器營收將季增雙位數。消費
電子方面將受惠新機推出與傳統旺季帶來的成長。群益預估
4Q26 營收 3.37 兆元，QoQ+11.7%；毛利率/營益率持續受 Vera 
Rubin 新平台出貨而稀釋，季減 0.10ppts/0.06ppts 來到
5.92%/3.77%，歸屬母公司淨利 899.02 億元，QoQ+15.97%，稅
後 EPS 6.39 元。 
Vera Rubin 接力 GB300，2027 年進入新平台主要貢獻期：管理
層預估 4Q26 Vera Rubin 出貨量將呈高雙位數增長。展望 2027
年，整體訂單並未隨近期市場的擔憂而調整，對於 AI 投資的展
望維持正向，預估 AI 機櫃出貨量將呈高雙位數成長。 
預估 2027 年營收 15.1 兆元，稅後 EPS 24.71 元：我們預估 2027
年營收 15.1 兆元，YoY+37.1%，毛利率 5.65%， YoY-0.4ppts； 
YoY-0.3ppts；歸屬母公司淨利 3,478.27 億元，YoY+25.4%，稅

2 群益投顧 
  ESG：   
環境：鴻海採用 TCFD 框架為基礎，全面盤點與評估氣候相關風險與機會，
並導入 ISO 14001 環境管理系統與 ISO 45001 職業安全衛生管理系統，同時
也推動「低碳供應鏈轉型推動計畫」，協助超過 30 家供應商完成節能專案，
累計減少碳排放約 15,000 公噸 CO₂e。鴻海已有超過 150 家供應商完成碳盤
查並簽署科學基礎減碳目標（SBTi），提升能源使用效率與減碳成效。  
社會：鴻海秉持「以人為本」理念，長期支持教育與青年培訓，設立「三創
育成基金會」，提供新創團隊資金與資源支持，自 2015 年以來已經培育超
過  100 組科技創業團隊，促進創新創業生態系發展，為社會培訓科技人才。
治理：董事會有設立「永續發展委員會」，主管由具備三年以上公開發行公
司財務主管經歷之財務長兼任，該委員自 2023 年起每年召開至少四次會議，
全體委員及相關主管亦於任職期間完成 ESG 專業訓練課程，強化永續治理
能力與資訊揭露透明度。 
公司簡介：  
鴻海精密工業股份有限公司（Hon Hai Precision Industry），成立於 1974 年，
由郭台銘創辦，總部位於新北市土城區。公司早期以模具及電子零組件製造
起家，逐步擴展至電子產品組裝、系統整合及設計製造服務，現為全球最大
電子專業製造服務（EMS）廠商。 
集團業務涵蓋消費智能產品、雲端網路產品、電腦終端產品及元件與其他產
品四大領域，旗下包含工業富聯（FII）、鴻騰精密（FIT）等重要子公司，
並透過全球事業單位及子公司分工，提供零組件、模組至整機系統的垂直整
合服務。集團生產及服務據點遍及全球逾 20 個國家及地區，包括中國、台
灣、印度、越南、墨西哥及美國等，全球員工人數於季節性高峰約達 100 萬
人。 
鴻海主要競爭對手包括廣達、緯創、和碩、仁寶及立訊精密等，依產品領域
有所不同。近年積極布局 AI 伺服器、電動車及半導體等新興業務，其中 AI
伺服器受惠全球雲端服務供應商（CSP）資本支出擴張，成為重要成長動能，
並持續鞏固全球電子製造服務龍頭地位。 
3Q26 營收 3.02 兆元，稅後 EPS 5.51 元： 3Q26 營收 3.02 兆元，QoQ+19%，
YoY+46%，毛利率 6.02%，QoQ-0.1ppts，YoY-0.33ppts，主要受產品組合及
原物料上漲影響導致毛利率下滑；營業利益 1,154.99 億元，QoQ+22%，
YoY+64%，營益率 3.83%， QoQ+0.07ppts， YoY+0.4ppts，營業利益成長高
於營收及營業毛利，顯現隨營運規模擴大，生產進入規模經濟；歸屬母公司
淨利 775.24 億元，QoQ+29%， YoY+34%，稅後 EPS 5.51 元。營業利益、淨
利和 EPS 皆創下歷史單季新高。 
10/2026 營收 MoM+12.7%，預估 4Q26 營收 QoQ+11.7%：鴻海 10/2026 營
收 1.31 兆元，MoM+12.7%， YoY+45.7%，創單月歷史新高，除受惠 GB300
持續放量外，iPhone 新機推出亦帶動營收顯著增長；Vera Rubin 僅小量出
貨。展望 4Q26，我們預期 GB200/300 出貨量季增持平至微幅下滑，Vera 
Rubin 則有雙位數以上的成長，並且由於 Vera Rubin 的 ASP 近乎倍增，預
估伺服器營收將季增雙位數。消費電子方面，我們認為記憶體與處理器成本

3 群益投顧 
上升，將使手機出貨面臨壓力，但產品 ASP 提升及 Apple 穩健的品牌效應
仍將為出貨量提供支撐，因此 4Q26 仍將受惠新機推出與傳統旺季帶來的成
長。相較於消費電子的季節性，GB200/300 放量與產品組合變化對全年營運
的影響更大，因此 2026 年的主要成長引擎仍是伺服器業務的擴張；Vera 
Rubin 則為 2027 年的成長建立基礎。綜上所述，群益預估 4Q26 營收 3.37
兆元，QoQ+11.7%；毛利率/營益率持續受 Vera Rubin 新平台出貨而稀釋，
下滑 0.10ppts/0.06ppts 來到 5.92%/3.77%，歸屬母公司淨利 899.02 億元，
QoQ+15.97%，稅後 EPS 6.39 元。 
雲端網路成為營運主軸，規模經濟支持本業獲利成長： 3Q26 雲端網路產品
占營收 54%，高於 2Q26 的 51%。消費智能、電腦終端及元件與其他產品分
別占 29%、12%及 5%。雲端網路產品受 AI 伺服器及機櫃出貨帶動，季增與
年增均強勁。消費智能產品亦受新品與傳統旺季帶動，季增優於公司原先預
期。據我們所知，伺服器約占雲端網路營收的 85%-90%，其中約 70%來自
AI 伺服器。隨雲端網路占比提高，AI 機櫃的交付時程、平台組合及物料認
列方式，將更直接影響鴻海的營收與獲利率。鴻海 3Q26 毛利率 6.02%，QoQ-
0.1ppts，主要受到整櫃 GPU 伺服器高昂的 BOM 所 稀 釋；營 業 利 益 率 3.83%，
QoQ+0.07ppts，雖然毛利率季減，營益率卻有所改善，反映出營收規模擴大
及營運效率提升的效益。 
Vera Rubin 接力 GB300，2027 年進入新平台主要貢獻期：AI 機櫃的新舊平
台交替是鴻海延續成長的核心動能。鴻海預計 2026 年 AI 伺服器機櫃出貨
較 2025 年倍增以上，且既有 GB 平台需求可能延續至 2027 年。此外，Vera 
Rubin 與 GB 系列同為 NVL72，因此我們預期鴻海將可較平滑的實現世代交
替。管理層預估 4Q26 Vera Rubin 出貨量將呈高雙位數增長。展望 2027 年，
伺服器業務上會新增更多 Neocloud 客戶，但主要客戶仍然是大型 CSP。整
體訂單並未隨近期市場的擔憂而調整，對於 AI 投資的展望維持正向，預估
AI 機櫃出貨量將呈高雙位數成長。 
自製零組件與自動化支撐獲利，高 BOM 仍主導毛利率走勢：鴻海透過垂直
整合提高單一專案可承接的製造內容，管理層表示除規模經濟外，零組件自
製率的提高與自動化生產都將為毛利率及營益率提供支撐。根據我們調查，
在晶片以外的零組件自製率約 50%，布局包括連接器、線材、Midplane、交
換器及散熱相關產品，零組件毛利率高於 15%，部分甚至可達 20%，管理層
亦提出 800G 以上交換器全年營收倍增的展望，這些產品有助鴻海由機櫃組
裝向高速互聯及關鍵元件延伸，並可提升附加價值。然而據我們了解目前鴻
海在 GPU 的大型專案中尚未採用 consignment model，記憶體等元件漲價則
主要透過 cost-plus 模式轉嫁給客戶，進入 Vera Rubin 世代後，由於 ASP 將
近倍增，會進一步造成毛利率稀釋，考慮到 GPU Rack 單價極高，我們預估
2027 年毛利率仍將承壓。 
ASIC 拓展產品組合，物料模式與 ASP 差異使營收比重低於出貨量佔比：據
我們了解，鴻海 ASIC 平台約佔 AI 伺服器營收的 20%，較 1H26 略升，且
ASIC 平台成長速度高於 GPU 平台，除既有客戶外亦有新專案，管理層預估
3Q26 ASIC 伺服器出貨量 YoY+150%，高於 GPU 伺服器 70%的出貨量年增。
我們認為 ASIC 專案增加可擴大鴻海在不同運算平台的參與，減少成長來源
集中於單一 GPU 平台的程度。此外，大部分 ASIC 專案將有望採用
consignment model，有助於緩解 AI 伺服器對於整體毛利率的負面效果，然
而 GPU 平台 ASP 顯著高於 ASIC 平台，因此 ASIC 的營收較難完整反映其

4 群益投顧 
專案及製造規模。我們認為目前 ASIC 專案採用 consignment model 尚不足
以顯著改善 GPU Rack 高 BOM 對整體毛利率的稀釋。 
策略投資三菱電機，電動車可望成為未來另一成長引擎：鴻海透過與三菱電
機的策略結盟深化在電動車領域的發展，雖投資金額小於 AI 相關的支出，
但將持續投入研發，目前已做好充足準備量產，將尋機大規模進入 EV 市場，
可望成為未來的另一成長動能。 
預估 2027 年營收 15.1 兆元，稅後 EPS 24.71 元：我們預估隨 AI 機櫃放量
將提高營收與獲利規模，2027 年營收 15.1 兆元，YoY+37.1%，毛利率 5.65%，
YoY-0.4ppts；營業利益 5,181.73 億元，YoY+25.5%，營益率 3.43%，YoY-
0.3ppts；歸屬母公司淨利 3,478.27 億元，YoY+25.4%，稅後 EPS 24.71 元。
Vera Rubin 由初期出貨轉為全年營收貢獻，加上既有 GB300 需求延續及
ASIC 業務擴張，帶動全年營收顯著增長。公司持續布局台灣、美國、墨西
哥及越南產能，亦有助因應客戶在不同地區的交付需求。 
投資建議：受惠 AI 伺服器的強勁需求，2026 AI 機櫃出貨可望倍數增長。
此外我們預期憑藉 iPhone 的品牌效應，4Q26 智慧型消費電子業務可望維持
過往的傳統出貨旺季。展望 2027 年，Vera Rubin 將成為主要成長引擎，主
要客戶對於 AI 伺服器的需求依舊強勁。關鍵製程自動化及零組件自製率的
提升亦有助提升營運效率，為毛利率及營益率提供部分支撐。考量上述原
因，我們給予鴻海 Trading Buy 的投資建議，目標價 272 元。

8 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

assist with process execution, which we think should shorten the learning curve. Nevertheless, depreciation is
expected to rise sharply from approximately NT$9.1bn in 2026 to NT$14–16bn in 2027. As a result, consolidated GM
could fall from above 32% in 2026 to roughly 25–30% in 2027, with gradual improvement throughout 2027 as
utilization improves. The established 8-inch business could reach the mid-30% range, while the new 12-inch may

remain at a 20–30% GM during its depreciation period.
• Market turbulence, recovery path and risks. VIS believes AI-related power-management demand remains intact
despite concerns about offshore computing restrictions for Chinese AI, project financing and possible changes in
GPU deployment. Power content rises with increasing compute density, faster signal transitions and more complex
voltage-management requirements across GPUs, ASICs, networking and cooling systems. For traditional consumer

electronics market, where high memory prices are raising notebook and smartphone costs and delaying replacement
demand. VIS has partly protected itself by cutting consumer capacity by more than 20% and reallocating it to AI and
server products. Other risks include higher substrate, chemical, gas, electricity and labor costs, as well as slower
Singapore qualification. Longer term GM should recover toward or above 30% in 2028–29 if the new fab reaches

targeted utilization, but the pace will depend on interposer volumes, IC yields and customer ramp schedules.

Vanguard International Semi.
Valuation
Our target price for VIS of NT$160 is based on 20x 2027E P/E, which we see as justified by its robust PMIC sales growth
momentum and increasing outflow order opportunity from TSMC with support from its consistent cash dividend policy. Our
target price equates to 2026/27E P/B of 3.8x/3.7x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) weaker 8-inch demand along with higher costs due to inflation or more severe

ASP competition from Chinese peers; 2) lower UTR of the new capacity at the newly acquired fab, with depreciation burden;
and 3) less-than-expected outsourcing opportunities from IDMs.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

1 
3597 映興(AVIN)

報告日期：2026/11/17 研 究 員：洪瑞陽 (Ryan Hung) 
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 資料中心新專案自 3Q26 開始放量，3Q26 營收 3.26 億元，QoQ 
+28.8%、YoY +39.6%，稅後 EPS 0.55 元；10 月營收進一步
提高至 1.30 億元，MoM +5.03%、YoY +76.69%，本研究預估
4Q26 營收 3.97 億元，下半年營運動能明顯高於上半年。 
 2027 年新專案進入完整年度貢獻，本研究預估台達電集團訂單
營收占比由 2025 年的 41.5%提升至 2026 年約 50%、2027 年
約 65%，成為未來兩年主要營收增量來源。 
 受惠新訂單放量，本研究預 2026／2027 年營收分別為 11.8 億
元／17.8 億元，2027 年 YOY+50.8%，新增業務以台達電相關
OEM 線束為主，毛利率將維持一定區間較難提升，預估 2026
／2027 年毛利率 23.9%／23.4%，獲利成長主要來自營收放
量，稅後 EPS 分別為 1.61／3.31 元。

一、2H26 新專案開始放量，營收動能明顯轉強 
3Q26 營收 3.26 億元，QoQ +28.8%、YoY +39.6%，毛利率 24.43%，
較 2Q26 維持前期水準；營業利益 2,993 萬元，營業利益率 9.17%，
較 2Q26 的 7.43%提高 1.74 個百分點，稅後淨利 2,156 萬元，EPS 
0.55 元。累計前三季營收 7.78 億元、EPS 0.90 元，營運較 2025
年同期明顯改善。

受惠資料中心新專案持續放量，7／8／9 月營收分別為 9,788 萬元
／1.05 億元／1.24 億元，呈現逐月成長。10 月營收進一步提升至
1.30 億元，MoM +5.03%、YoY +76.69%，創單月新高。本研究依
10 月實際營收、既有業務營運水準及新專案出貨進度推估，4Q26
營收約 3.97 億元，QoQ +21.8%，毛利率 23.65%，營業利益率
11.1%，稅後 EPS 約 0.71 元，下半年營運成長動能持續增強。

二、AI 電力需求成長，新專案放量，2027 年全年貢獻 
AI 算力需求提升帶動資料中心電力密度增加，電源架構朝高功
率、高電流及高可靠度方向發展，HVDC、PDU、UPS／BBU 及
PSU 等電源與備援系統需求同步增加。映興產品已涵蓋 AI 資料中
心機櫃、備援電力模組、基地台機房及電源供應系統等線束模組，
公司產品亦可延伸至大電流與高可靠度電力傳輸應用。

映興在 AI 資料中心的供應模式以電源系統相關線束及連接元件為

2 
主，並非直接供應 CSP，因此營運成長主要隨下游客戶資料中心
電源系統出貨放大。本研究預期 2026 年新專案僅貢獻部分年度，
2027 年除具全年化效果外，出貨仍持續 Ramp，因此預估新專案
相關營收較 2026 年成長逾 2 倍，帶動台達電集團訂單營收占比由
2025 年的約四成提高至 2026 年約 50%、2027 年約 65%，成為未
來兩年主要營收增量來源。

三、既有應用維持成長，較高毛利產品支撐產品組合 
除 AI 資料中心外，公司既有產品橫跨工業應用、移動載具、車載
電子及儲能等領域。移動載具涵蓋無人機、機器狗、自行車、摩
托車、滑板車及船外機等所需的防水連接器與線束模組；車載電
子則供應車用風扇、OBC 及煞車系統線束；工業應用包括氫燃料
電池、馬達、機械手臂與家電相關線束及代理元件。

公司自有防水連接器累積 93 項國內外專利，具微型大電流、高插
拔次數、IP68 防水及抗火花等特性；OEM 線束則可透過自主運算
工具進行阻抗與結構優化設計，並取得 ISO 9001、ISO 14001、IATF 
16949 及 UL 等認證。

本研究預期車載及移動載具等較高毛利產品持續成長，可部分抵
銷 AI 資料中心新增 OEM 線束占比提高造成的毛利率負向影響。
3Q26 實際毛利率仍達 24.43%，顯示新增訂單放量尚未造成毛利率
明顯下降；惟隨 2027 年 OEM 營收占比進一步提高，產品組合仍
有毛利率些許負向影響。因此映興未來兩年營運型態將呈現「營
收快速成長、毛利率大致維持、費用率下降」，獲利成長主要來
自營收規模放大，而非毛利率擴張。

四、預估 2026／2027 年稅後 EPS 1.61／3.31 元 
2026 年受惠資料中心新專案自 2H26 開始貢獻，本研究預估全年
營收 11.75 億元，YoY +36.5%；毛利率 23.9%、營業費用約 1.82
億元，費用率由 2025 年的 17.4%降至 15.5%，帶動營業利益約 9,837
萬元、營業利益率提升至 8.37%，EPS 1.61 元。

2027 年新專案進入完整年度貢獻，本研究預估全年營收 17.75 億
元，YoY +51.1%；毛利率 23.4%、營業費用約 2.20 億元，費用率
進一步降至 12.4%，營業利益約 1.95 億元、營業利益率 11.0%。
預估稅後淨利約 1.31 億元，EPS 3.31 元，YoY 約+106%。 
五、投資建議：買進，目標價 59 元 
隨台達電訂單新專案貢獻，映興有新的營運動能，帶動營收與 EPS
正向成長，以 2027 年 EPS3.31 計算，根據歷史區間與同業評價，
給予 18 倍本益比，目標價 59 元

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況 
一、公司簡介 
映興電子成立於 1986 年，主要從事連接線束模組、代理元件及自
有防水連接器，營運據點涵蓋台灣、中國深圳與昆山、泰國及美
國。公司產品可分為三類：第一為 OEM／ODM／OBM 連接線束
模組；第二為代理元件，包括 Sensata 溫度保護元件、日本 Kurabe
特殊電線及 Anderson Power 大電流連接器；第三為自有防水連接
器。公司目前聚焦 AI 伺服器、工業應用、移動載具、車載電子及
綠能儲能五大應用市場。

公司核心能力在於複雜線束加工、防水連接器設計及代理元件整
合。自有防水連接器累積 93 項國內外專利，具備 IP68 防水、抗
火花、高插拔次數及微型大電流等特性；OEM 線束則可透過自主
運算工具進行阻抗與結構優化設計，並取得 ISO 9001、ISO 14001、
IATF 16949 及 UL 等認證。相較一般單純線材加工廠，公司可利
用一線品牌代理權作為客戶導入入口，再延伸至線束加工與自有
連接器，提高單一客戶合作深度。

五大應用中，AI 伺服器主要供應資料中心 HVDC／PDU／CDU 電
源機櫃、UPS／BBU 備援電池櫃、TPS 基地台機房及 PSU 內部線
束；工業應用涵蓋氫燃料電池、馬達、機械手臂及家電所需線束
與代理元件；移動載具包括無人機、機器狗、自行車、摩托車、
滑板車及船外機所需防水連接器與線束；車載電子則供應車用風
扇、OBC 及煞車系統線束；綠能儲能則以太陽能儲能、大電流連
接器及電池櫃線束為主，相關技術亦可延伸至 AI 資料中心 BBU
備援電力應用。

二、AI 新專案放量，台達電集團訂單營收占比持續提升 
AI 資料中心為映興目前最重要的新增成長來源。隨 AI 算力需求
提升，資料中心電力系統逐步朝高功率、高電流與高可靠度方向
發展，HVDC、PDU、UPS／BBU 及 PSU 等電源與備援系統需求
同步增加。公司目前相關產品已涵蓋 AI 資料中心機櫃、備援電力
模組、基地台機房及電源供應系統等線束模組，因此可隨下游資
料中心電源系統需求擴張而受惠。公司亦已具備 UPS 電池系統、
IT Rack、PDU 及電池模組串接等產品經驗，可延伸至 AI 資料中
心大電流及高可靠度電力傳輸應用。

公司並非直接供應 CSP，而是透過台達電集團訂單切入終端雲端
服務業者供應鏈。映興供應內容集中於資料中心電源系統內部線
束與連接元件，因此營運節奏與台達電集團資料中心電源業務高
度連動。除了資料中心電源機櫃外，公司在台達電集團既有業務
亦涵蓋 PSU、車載充電系統、基地台機房、儲能櫃及自動化設備
等產品，客戶合作範圍並非僅限於 AI 資料中心單一應用。

本研究估算，2025 年台達電集團訂單營收占比約四成；2026 年上
半年新專案尚未正式進入主要營收貢獻期，因此相關營收仍以
TPS、車用及既有電源產品為主。隨資料中心新專案於 3Q26 開始
放量，7、8、9 月公司合併營收逐月提升，3Q26 營收達 3.26 億元，
QoQ +28.8%。10 月營收進一步增加至 1.30 億元，MoM +5.03%、
YoY +76.69%，本研究預期台達電集團訂單相關營收成長動能自下
半年起明顯轉強。

本研究對資料中心新專案採 Bottom-up 推估，將台達電集團訂單
營收區分為既有業務與資料中心新增業務，並依已公告營收、既
有業務正常營運水準及後續專案 Ramp 節奏進行推估，而非直接
以客戶占比反推公司營收。由於 2026 年新專案僅貢獻部分年度，

2027 年同時享有全年化與出貨增加兩項效果，本研究預估相關新
專案營收較 2026 年成長逾 2 倍，帶動台達電集團訂單營收占比由
2026 年約 50%進一步提高至 2027 年約 65%，為未來兩年最主要
營收增量來源。

惟台達電集團訂單相關業務主要仍以 OEM 線束為主，毛利率相對
低於自有防水連接器、車載及部分移動載具產品，因此台達電集
團訂單營收占比快速提升將對產品組合形成一定毛利壓力。不
過，3Q26 毛利率仍達 24.43%，維持前期水準，加上集團既有產能
可因應新增訂單，營收規模放大有助於固定製造成本攤提。本研
究預估 2026／2027 年毛利率約 23.9%／23.4%，營業利益率則由
8.3%提高至 11.0%，顯示本輪成長主要來自 AI 帶動營收放量及費
用率下降，而非毛利率成長。 
三、車載與移動載具高毛利產品及其他業務正向發展 
移動載具產品涵蓋無人機、機器狗、自行車、摩托車、滑板車、
輪椅及船外機所需的防水連接器與線束模組。公司在此領域主要
競爭力來自自有防水連接器，可提供 IP68、防水、抗火花及高插
拔次數設計，適合戶外、震動及高濕度等環境，產品附加價值高
於一般 OEM 線束。公司原有兩輪載具為既有核心市場，近年亦逐
步向無人機與四足機器人等新應用延伸。

考量自行車庫存逐步正常化、無人機非紅供應鏈需求提高，以及
機器狗等新型移動載具應用擴張，本研究預估移動載具中短期營
收可維持中個位數成長，並以約 5～8%作為 2027 年相關業務成長
假設。由於此類產品較多採用公司自有防水連接器及客製化線
束，其毛利結構優於一般 OEM 產品，因此除營收成長外，亦可作
為未來產品組合的重要支撐。

車載電子方面，公司主要供應車用風扇、On-board Charger 及煞車
系統線束，已取得 IATF 16949 車用認證，可切入對品質、可靠度
及認證要求較高的汽車供應鏈。公司現有產品已涵蓋車載充電、
煞車系統及相關電力電子應用，隨全球汽車朝電動化與智慧化發
展，電力電子、充電系統及熱管理需求仍具中長期成長趨勢。

考量車用產品認證週期較長、導入後客戶黏著度相對較高，加上
電動車及高階車用電子滲透率持續提升，本研究預估車載電子中
長期營收成長約 10～15%。部分高階車用產品專案能見度亦延伸
至未來數年，預期車載業務可持續成為公司較穩定、且毛利率優
於一般 OEM 線束的產品線。

工業應用為公司既有營收的重要來源，產品涵蓋氫燃料電池、馬

達、機械手臂、燃油引擎所需線束模組與代理元件，以及空調壓
縮機、吹風機、吸塵器等家電使用的代理元件；客戶應用橫跨燃
料電池、工業自動化與消費型家電。

本研究預期工業應用仍受惠工業自動化、燃料電池及資料中心相
關電力需求，維持穩健成長。不過須注意公司應用別與客戶別分
類並非完全對應，部分 AI 資料中心及基礎建設相關產品亦可能歸
類於工業應用，因此本研究在財測時不直接套用單一工業應用成
長率，以避免與台達電集團訂單 AI 專案重複計算。

綠能儲能方面，公司產品包括太陽能儲能系統、大電流儲能連接
器及電池櫃串接線束，其技術與 AI 資料中心 BBU 在大電流傳輸、
電池模組串接及高可靠度連接等方面具有共通性。隨儲能與資料
中心備援電力需求增加，公司過去累積的電池櫃及大電流連接技
術可同時支援傳統儲能及 AI 資料中心相關應用，提供中長期新增
業務機會。

整體而言，本研究預期其他客戶業務將由車載、移動載具、工業
及儲能等既有應用共同支撐。2026 年在既有應用回升下，預估其
他客戶營收約 5.86 億元，YoY 約+16.5%；考量 2027 年基期提高，
且工業分類中部分 AI 營收已另於台達電集團訂單模型估算，本研
究採較保守成長假設，預估 2027 年其他客戶營收約 6.27 億元，
YoY 約+7%。此部分雖非未來兩年最大營收增量來源，但較高毛
利產品比重有助於抵銷 AI OEM 線束占比上升造成的產品組合壓
力。 
叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估 
一、預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 1.61 元 
本研究將營收拆分為「台達電集團訂單」與「其他客戶」兩部分。
台達電集團訂單再區分既有業務及資料中心新專案；其他客戶則
依車載、移動載具、工業及儲能等既有應用的實際營運水準與成
長趨勢估算。台達電集團訂單營收占比為兩部分個別預估後除算
得出，而非預先假設占比後回推營收。

3Q26 營收 3.26 億元，QoQ +28.8%、YoY +39.6%，較本研究原預
估 3.22 億元高約 1.5%；毛利率 24.43%，優於原預估 23.2%，惟營
業費用 4,976 萬元，亦高於原預估 4,200 萬元，營業利益 2,993 萬
元、營業利益率 9.17%，稅後 EPS 0.55 元。雖然營收與毛利率優
於原預估，但費用同步增加，顯示新增訂單放量後仍需考量營運
規模擴大所帶來的費用成長。

10 月營收 1.30 億元，MoM +5.03%、YoY +76.69%，亦高於本研究

原估約 1.23 億元。考量資料中心新專案出貨進度優於原先預期，
本研究上修 4Q26 營收至 3.97 億元，QoQ +21.8%。毛利率考量 3Q26
實際維持 24%以上，且產能利用率提高有助於固定製造成本攤
提，預估 4Q26 毛利率 24.0%；營業費用則依 3Q26 實際水準估約
5,000 萬元，營業利益約 4,534 萬元、營業利益率 11.4%，稅 後 EPS
約 0.73 元。下半年營收合計約 7.24 億元，較上半年增加約 60.3%。

本研究預估 2026 全年營收 11.75 億元，YoY +36.5%，其中台達電
集團訂單營收約 5.89 億元，占營收比重約 50%；其他客戶營收約
5.86 億元，YoY 約+16.5%，維持雙位數成長。

雖新增台達電集團訂單以 OEM 線束為主，產品組合對毛利率形成
一定的負向影響，但 3Q26 實際毛利率仍維持 24.43%，反映營收
規模增加可部分抵銷產品組合影響。本研究預估 2026 年毛利率約
23.9%，較 2025 年的 23.6%小幅提升。營業費用預估約 1.82 億元，
YoY 約+21.9%，增幅低於營收，費用率由 2025 年的 17.4%下降至
15.5%，帶動營業利益約 1.00 億元、營業利益率提升至 8.37%。

業外損益預估約-598 萬元，考量前三季實際稅率及 4Q26 有效稅率
35%的假設，預估全年有效稅率約 31.7%，稅後淨利約 6,404 萬元，
EPS 1.63 元。獲利成長主要來自營收放量、固定成本攤提改善及
營業費用率下降，而非單純依賴毛利率提升。

二、預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 3.31 元  
2027 年營收成長主要來自資料中心新專案。由於 2026 年新專案僅
自下半年開始貢獻，2027 年除完整貢獻全年外，出貨仍持續 Ramp 
up。本研究考量 3Q26 實際營收與 10 月營收均優，預估新專案相
關營收較 2026 年成長逾 2 倍；既有台達電集團訂單業務則維持穩
定，預估全年台達電集團訂單營收約 11.48 億元。

其他客戶方面，本研究預估 2026 年營收約 5.86 億元，2027 年考
量車載、移動載具及其他既有業務持續成長，但 2026 已有較高基
期，因此採較正常化成長假設，預估全年營收約 6.27 億元，YoY
約+7%。

兩者加總後，預估 2027 年營收 17.75 億元，YoY +51.1%，台達電
集團訂單營收占比提高至約 65%。相較原預估 16.94 億元，本次主
要反映資料中心新專案出貨進度提前，而非 ASP 上升。

季度營收預估呈逐季上升，1Q27／2Q27／3Q27／4Q27 分別為 4.05
／4.29／4.58／4.83 億元。新專案出貨增加使台達電集團訂單營收
占比逐步提高，但考量 OEM 比重上升，毛利率維持；另一方面，

的 10.7%逐步提高至 4Q27 的 11.3%，顯示本輪獲利成長仍主要由
營業槓桿帶動。 
三、台達電集團訂單為主要成長，其他業務維持穩健  
台達電集團訂單方面，本研究預估既有業務維持正常營運水準，
資料中心新專案則自 2H26 進入放量階段。考量 3Q26 實際營收及
10 月營收均優，預估 2026／2027 年台達電集團訂單營收分別為
5.89／11.48 億元，YoY 約+65%／+95%，占合併營收比重由 2026
年的約 50%提高至 2027 年約 65%，為未來兩年最主要營收增量來
源。

其他客戶方面，本研究依公司既有應用營運趨勢進行估算，預估
2026 年營收約 5.86 億元，2027 年增加至約 6.27 億元，YoY 約+7%。
2027 年增速較 2026 正常化，主要考量 2026 已有較高基期。

현대차 (005380)

26.3Q Review: 이제 올라가야 할 때 Company Brief

본업 Review: 26.3Q 매출액 45.1조원, 영업이익 2.6조원  
현대차는 26.3Q 매출액 45.1원 (-3.4% YoY, -8.3% QoQ), 영업이익 2.6조원 (+5.5% 
YoY, -6.1% QoQ, +5.9% OPM) 등의 실적을 시현했다 [iM증권 추정치 매출액: 
45.3조원, 영업이익: 2.4조원]. 매출액은 시장 컨센서스에 부합했고, 영업이익의 경우 
시장 컨센서스였던 2.4조원을 소폭 상회했다. 주요 구성 요소 별 내용은 아래와 같다.  
① P: 26.3Q HEV 판매 대수는 16.6만대를 기록하며 전사 판매 대비 비중 
18.2% 달성 (+18.8% YoY, -11.1% QoQ). 미국의 경우 국내 공장 생산 
감소에 따른 수출 부진으로 도매 판매는 소폭 감소했으나, 판매 비중은 
25.2%로 전반적인 믹스는 직전 분기와 유사. 26.3Q 평균환율이 직전 분기 
대비 크게 하락했으나, 전년 동기 기저 탓에 오히려 + 환율 효과 관찰됨. 
② Q: 26.3Q 도매 판매 대수는 91.8만대로 전년 동기 대비 11.5% 감소 (이하 
YoY 기준, 미국: -10.0%, 유럽: -18.1% 국내: -27.2% 인도: +18.3%). 
26.2Q 안전 공업 화재에 이어 26.3Q에 발생한 파업의 영향으로 생산 
대수는 감소. 국내 공장에서의 생산 차질은 내수 판매에 더해 수출 감소, 즉 
전체적인 판매 축소로 연결됨. 26.4Q부터 생산 관련 노이즈는 발생하지 
않을 전망. 이에 더해 아반떼, 투싼 등의 신차 판매 효과를 통한 반등 가능.  
③ C: 26.3Q 관세 비용은 직전 분기와 유사한 수준으로 발생한 것으로 파악 
(25.2Q 0.8조원, 25.3Q 1.8조원, 25.4Q 1.5조원, 26.1Q 0.8조원, 26.2Q 
0.9조원). 이란 사태에 따른 주요 원자재 가격 상승 압력은 계속 존재하나, 
내부 원가 절감 정책으로 약 5,000억원의 원가 상승분을 상쇄. 현대차는 
26.4Q 원가 절감 정책의 효과가 더 크게 나타날 것이라고 언급. 26.3Q 
기말 환율이 전년 동기 대비 급락함에 따라 판매보층충당부채전입액 또한 
감소 (26.3Q 매출원가, 판매관리비 각 전년 동기 대비 1.7%, 19.2% 감소). 
금융 부문의 경우 26.3Q 매출액 9.1조원 (+27.8% YoY), 영업이익 4,710억원 (-
18.2% YoY, -37.9%) 등의 실적을 기록했다. 완성차 판매가 전반적으로 
위축되었음에도 외형 성장을 이어갔으나, 금리 상승에 따른 이자 비용 등 전반적인 
조달 관련 비용이 증가했다. 일부 EV를 중심으로 중고차 가격이 소폭 하락한 점 또한 
HCA 등 주요 부문 이익 감소의 배경이다. 26.3Q 현대차 순이익은 1,932억원을 
기록했는데, 이는 영업외손익이 전년 동기 789억원에서 229억원으로 감소했기 
때문이다. 현대차는 주요 환율 하락에 따른 외화환산손실 증가의 영향이라고 밝혔다. 
1) 생산 단에서의 노이즈가 연속해 발생하며 내수 판매 및 수출 감소가 불가피했으나, 
2) 원자재 가격 상승 영향을 내부 원가 절감 정책을 통해 상쇄했고, 3) 주요 
평균환율이 하락했으나 기말환율 또한 급락하며 판매보증충당부채전입액 감소 효과가 
나타나 시장 기대를 소폭 상회하는 수익성이 관찰되었다는 것이 26.3Q 실적 총평이다. 
현대차는 2026년 도매 판매 가이던스를 416만대에서 400만대로 하향 조정했다. 
26.3Q 누적 도매 판매 대수는 288만대이기에 하향 조정은 불가피했다. 단 신규 
가이던스에 따르면 26.4Q 판매 목표는 약 110만대 수준이라는 것인데, 아반떼, 투싼, 
주요 모델 페이스리프트, GV80 HEV 등의 신차 효과가 얼마나 나타날지가 관건일 
것이다 (이하 분기 도매 판매, 25.1Q 100.1만대, 25.2Q 105.7만대, 25.3Q 103.8만대).

현대차 (005380)
2
미래 사업 업데이트: 자율주행은 계획대로, Atlas는 조금씩 변신중
26.3Q 컨퍼런스 콜에서 특별히 SDV~Boston Dynamics 관련 업데이트는 확인되지
않았으나 여러 매체를 통해 파악된 현대차그룹의 미래 사업 진행 상황은 다음과 같다.
① SDV/자율주행: 데이터 플라이 휠에 대한 중요성 강조. 27년 말부터 광주
권역 자율주행 실증 사업에 투입되는 차량은 Atria AI 활용. SDV 플랫폼이
적용된 차량 양산 시작은 29년. 양산 SDV 출하량 증가함에 따라 29년부터
GPU 5만개 이상으로 구성되는 새만금 데이터센터를 자체 운영을 목표함.
박민우 포티투닷 사장 겸 AVP 본부장이 부임한 이후 Tesla, nVIDIA 출신의
개발 인력을 충원 중인 상황. 26.05 그랜저 페이스리프트 모델에 플레오스
탑재 시작. 26.09부터 판매되고 있는 신형 아반떼에도 플레오스 탑재 완료.

② Boston Dynamics: 10.8 Rohit Prasad를 CEO로 선임. Rohit Prasad은
아마존에서 Alex 개발에 중추적인 역할을 수행했던 인물. 현대차그룹에서
직접 하드웨어 개발 및 양산을 챙기고, 소프트웨어에 역량을 높이겠다는
행보로 판단. 26.10 Atlas의 신규 덱스트러스를 공개했는데, 기존 제품 대비
자유도가 크게 증가 (7DoF→14DoF). 순수 직구동 방식을 여전히
고수함.10.27 Boston Dynamics가 주요 부품 업체에게 Atlas 1만대 물량의
부품 PO를 곧 발송할 것이라는 기사가 보도됨. 28년까지 연 3만대 CAPA의
공장 완공이라는 기존 계획 대비 양산 및 개발 속도가 빨라진다는 시그널임.
현대차그룹의 미래 사업 업데이트를 또 찾아볼 수 있을 가장 빠른 이벤트는 CES
2027이다. 다만 CES 2027에는 현대차가 아닌 그룹사가 참여함에 따라 해당 행사에서

유의미한 업데이트 내용은 찾아보기 힘들 것이며 이에 중장기 관점의 접근이 필요하다.
시장의 우려가 반영된 주가 하락: 빨라질 속도와 올라올 본업에 주목
현대차 주가는 26.2H 접어들며 크게 하락하고 있고, CES 2026 전후의 상승폭을 사실상
모두 반납했다 (연초 기준 -1.3%, 26.2H 이후 -41.7%). 또한 최근 시장 반등
흐름에서도 사실상 소외되고 있다. 현대차를 바라보는 투자자들의 우려는 아래와 같다.
첫째, CID 전후로 Boston Dynamics 관련 업데이트가 너무 부족하다는 것이다. 26.08
현대차 CID 행사를 앞두고 많은 투자자들은 빅테크 업체들의 지분 투자 및 Atlas
개발과 관련해 새로운 내용을 기대한 것이 사실이다. 이런 상황에서 유럽, 내수 등 주요
지역 에서의 판매 업황 또한 좋지 않다 보니, 시장의 실망은 더욱 컸다. 전술한 것과
같이 CES 2027에 현대차가 참여하지 않는다는 점에서 내년에 대한 기대도 크지 않다.

둘째, 내수와 유럽 업황이 그룹사 대비 너무 좋지 못하다는 점이다. 26.3Q 현대차 내수,
유럽 도매 판매는 전년 동기 대비 각 27.2%, 18.1% 감소했다. 반면 26.3Q 기아 내수,
유럽 도매 판매는 전년 동기 대비 각 2.2%, 18.7% 증가했다. 특히 유럽에서의 부진은
EV 라인업이 상대적으로 빈약하고, 중국 업체와의 경쟁이 심화되고 있다는 구조적
문제에서 출발하기에, 투자자들은 26년을 넘어 27, 28년의 성장까지 우려하고 있다
단 우리는 Boston Dynamics의 Atlas는 양산이라는 방향성이 설정된지 오래되지
않았다는 점을 기억해야 한다. 초기 단계부터 양산을 위해 개발된 Tesla Optimus 대비
개발 속도가 빠를 수는 없다. 1~2만대 수준의 데모 제품이 확보되고, 이를 바탕으로
데이터 수집이 일정 부분 확보되는 이후부터 관련 사업 확대 속도는 빨라 질것이다.

취약 지역 판매 업황의 경우에도 내수를 시작으로 점진적으로 개선될 것이다. 생산 차질
외에도 Tesla 및 중국 OEM들의 시장 침투가 우려됨은 분명하지만, 주력 차종 중에서도
국내 판매 볼륨이 큰 아반떼와 투싼의 신차 효과는 분명 기대되는 지점이다. 유럽에서의
반등은 시간이 필요할 것이나, 일단 아이오닉 3가 26.4Q부터 본격적으로 판매될
예정이다. 단 이는 큰 변동 없이 IAA가 2028년부터 시행된다는 전제에 근거하고 있다.
현대차 목표주가 600,000원 유지: 여전한 Multiple 할증 요인
현대차 목표주가를 600,000원으로 유지한다. 26년 EPS 기준 6~8배 수준의 P/E
Multiple을 부여받고 있는 Peer Group들은 중국 시장 M/S 축소로 수익성이 급락하고
있고, 미래 사업 경쟁력은 여전히 전무하다. 현대차의 Multiple 할증 요인은 충분하다.

그림17. 유럽 생산 OEM 생산원가 추이 
 `  
자료: iM증권 리서치본부

IAA는 EU판 IRA로 볼 수 있음. 현대차그룹의 경우 현대차 (터키, 체코)와 기아 (슬로바키아) 모두 유렵 현지
생산 라인을 이미 확보했고, 현대모비스를 중심으로 한 부품 자급률 비중 이미 높음. 또한 주요 부품에 있어
FTA 체결국의 원산지를 EU산과 동일하게 적용하는 규정 존재. 실제 발효까지 큰 변수가 없다면 국내 완성차
업체의 경우 구조적으로 수혜 클 것. 단 중국 업체들에 대한 명시적 배제 규정이 없다는 점은 한계 중 하나. 
IAA 주요 조항 주요 내용
적용 대상
공공조달 차량, 공적 지원(구매 보조금 등)을 받는 차량, 법인차가 대상임. 전기
차, PHEV, 수소차에 적용됨. 2028년부터 회원국은 무공해 및 저공해이면서 EU
산인 차량이 아니면 법인차를 지원할 수 없음. 판매 금지가 아니라 수요 측 혜택
을 역내 생산과 연동하는 구조
역내 부품 요건
1단계(발효 6개월 후)는 EU 조립, 배터리 제외 부품가치 70% EU산, 셀 포함
배터리 핵심부품 3개 EU산을 요구함. 2단계(1단계 3년 후)는 배터리 핵심부품
5개(셀, 양극재,BMS 포함)와 전동 파워트레인·전장 가치 50% EU산이 추가됨.
소형차 특례
전장 4.2m 이하 전기 승용차가 대상임. EU 조립에 더해 부품가치 70%와
배터리 핵심부품 3개 중 하나만 충족하면 됨. 충족 시 CO2 규제상 1.3대로 인정
됨(2025~34년).
FDI 조건 1억 유로 초과 투자가 대상이며, 지분 49% 상한, EU 인력 50% 이상, EU산 투 입재 30% 이상 등의 조건이 붙음.
FTA·관세동맹국 예외
FTA나 관세동맹을 맺은 국가의 부품은 EU산과 동등한 부품으로 인정되며,
정부조달협정(GPA) 가입국도 일부 조항에 포함됨. 단 최종 조립국까지 인정되
는지는 불명확하며, 조문상으로는 EU, EEA 역내 조립만 인정되는 것으로 해석
됨. 따라서 한국산 부품은 EU산으로 인정 가능하나, 한국이나 튀르키예에서 조
립한 완성차는 요건 미충족 가능성이 높음.
FTA 예외 배제 조건
집행위는 위임법으로 특정국을 동등 대상에서 제외할 수 있음. 해당국이 EU 기업
에 내국민 대우를 하지 않는 경우, 공급망 의존도나 공급 안보 위협이 있는 경우,
해당 협정의 예외 조항에 해당하는 경우가 배제 사유임. 중국은 FTA가 없어 애초
에 대상이 아님.
2020 2021 2022 2023
대당 생산원가 30,683 32,029 35,079 38,140
대당 판매가격 34,404 27,557 31,244 33,560
1인당 인건비 70,981 74,904 78,348 88,198
통상적으로 중국 OEM의 생산 원가 (중국 생산 가정)은 글로벌 OEM 대비 30% 낮다고 추정됨. BYD의 경우 돌핀 등과 같
은 C 세그먼트 차량에서의 유럽 수익성이 낮지만, A~B 세그먼트 급의 돌핀 서프의 경우 매우 높은 수익성 시현. 이는 유럽
시장 내의 A~B 세그먼트 경쟁 강도가 낮다는 점에서 기인. 즉 100% 수준의 관세가 부과되지 않는다면 BYD의 가격 경쟁
력은 유지되는 것. 따라서 EU는 2,000유로 수준의 중국 OEM의 대당 이익을 상쇄하기 위해 이에 준하는 수준으로 지급되
는 EV 구매 보조금 및 법인 차량 세제혜택을 검토 중인 것이고, 이를 IAA에 담아낼지가 핵심.
유럽 생산 OEM 생산원가 및 판매가격 추이

인도: GM 탈레가온 공장 인수를 통한 생산량 확대에 나설 예정이고, 이
를 통한 인도 내수 수요 대응 및 글로벌 신흥 시장 진출 또한 목표

통해 EV 수요 회복 국면을 앞두고 있는 유럽 시장 대응에 나설 예정

고 있음. 스포티지 및 셀토스 등 HEV를 통한 기존 차급 대응 강화 전망

당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상 
산업추천 투자등급

 Overweight(비중확대) 
 Neutral(중립) 
 Underweight(비중축소)

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점불확실성보다 성장_3801b631.pdf

Date: 未知日期
Pages: 4

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
3분기 실적 컨센서스 상회 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 매출액은 1조 4,903억 원(+18.5% YoY), 
영업이익은 6,401억 원(+1.0% YoY, 영업이익률 43.0%)으로 시장 컨센서스를 각각 
10%, 5% 상회했다. 1~4공장의 풀가동이 이어진 가운데 5공장의 인증용 생산배치 
물량과 미국 락빌 공장 매출(약 1,000억 원)이 처음으로 반영되며 외형 성장을 
견인했다. 매출원가율은 50.4%로 전년 동기(41.4%), 전분기(47.0%) 대비 
상승했는데, 락빌 공장과 5공장의 초기 물량 반영에 따른 것으로 판단한다. 향후 
물량 상승에 따라 매출원가율은 감소할 것으로 예상한다. 그럼에도 EBITDA 
마진율은 50.4%로 높은 수준을 유지하며 7,515억 원을 달성했다. 순이익은 원/달러 
환율 하락에 따른 외화환산손실이 반영되며 3,911억 원을 기록했다.  
환율 하락에도 가이던스 상향하며 성장 궤도 유지 
2026년 연간 매출액은 5조 6,133억 원(+23.2% YoY), 영업이익은 2조 4,605억 
원(+18.9% YoY)으로 전망한다. 최근 원/달러 환율 하락에도 이번 실적발표에서 
상향 제시한 연간 매출 성장률 20% 이상은 충분히 달성 가능할 것으로 판단한다. 
4분기에는 락빌 공장의 매출 약 1,000억 원과 5공장 가동 확대에 더해, 5월 
파업으로 이연된 물량의 매출 인식이 성장에 기여할 전망이다.  
투자의견 매수 및 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
비만 치료제 CDMO 진출을 위한 폴리펩타이드 그룹 인수는 공개매수 응모율 
87.88%로 최소 조건을 충족해 11월 말 완료를 앞두고 있어 신규 성장 동력은 
확보된 것으로 판단한다. 노사 리스크도 법원의 쟁의행위 제한 확대에 이어 노조가 
2차 총파업을 잠정 연기하며 크게 완화됐다. 미국 관세 불확실성으로 부진했던 신규 
수주 모멘텀은 올해 연말부터 내년에 걸쳐 강화될 것으로 전망하며, 불확실성 
해소와 성장 기대감을 반영할 시점이라 판단한다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

2026.10.29   
HD현대중공업  (329180)

3Q26: 조선사는 우직해야 한다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 5조 7,536억원/영업이익 8,992억원 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 매출은 5조 7,536억원/영업이익은 8,992억원으로 
컨센서스(6조 1,295억원/9,671억원) 및 당사 추정치(6조 859억원/9,679억원) 대비 
매출 및 영업이익 각각 -6.1%/-7.0%, -5.5%/-7.1%를 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다.  
조선사업부 매출이 4조 3,988억원/영업이익 6,463억원(OPM 14.7%)이며 이중 상선 
OPM은 14.7%로, 2분기 17.8%에 비해 3.1%P 하락하였으나 1분기 15.9%와 크게 
차이나지 않는 수준을 유지하면서 조업일수 및 환율 하락에도 불구하고 P-MIX및 
빈티지 개선에 따른 효과를 보여주었다. 해양사업부는 매출 3,799억원/영업이익 
1,265억원(OPM 33.3%)로 지난 분기(15.9%)보다도 훨씬 높은 영업이익을 
기록하였다. 엔진기계사업부는 매출 9,511억원/영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)를 
기록하며 역시 견조한 실적을 기록하였다.  
일회성 이익으로 Trion 공사의 C/O 180억원, 일회성 손실로는 셧다운에 따른 
조업감소 영향 500억원 및 환율하락에 따른 이익 감소분 450억원이 있었으며 금액은 
공개되지 않았으나 임단협 잠정타결에 따른 성과급/타결금 지급에 대한 추가 충당금의 
75% 인식이 반영되었다. 알려진 일회성만 제거하더라도 실질 영업이익률은 
17%이상으로 2분기 16.4%보다 높아진 호실적이라고 할 수 있다. 특히 RUYA 공사가 
본 궤도에 오르면서 해양사업부의 이익률이 괄목할 만큼 높아졌으며 지속가능하다는 
회사의 설명이다.

투자의견 BUY 및 조선업종 TOP PICK 유지, 목표주가 530,000원 하향 
HD현대중공업의 목표주가를 530,000원으로 38.3% 하향하지만, 투자의견 BUY와 
조선업종 TOP PICK을 유지한다. 환율 하락 및 28년 하반기 엔진증설 매출확대, 
해양공사 이익률 상승 등 요인을 종합 반영하여 실적 추정치를 미세 반영하여 도출한 
2027F BPS 131,910원에 Target P/B를 기존대비 하향한 4.0배로 적용하였다. 특수선 
기대감 및 선가상승 기대감 하락에 따른 조선업종 전반의 밸류에이션 하락을 반영하여 
Target을 낮췄지만 그럼에도 조선업종 중 가장 높은 멀티플을 부여하며 Top 
Pick의견을 견지한다. 여러 악재에도 불구하고 HD현대중공업은 탄탄하다. 본업인 
상선사업부의 9월말 기준 수주량은 137.9억 달러로 목표치를 이미 초과 달성하였으며 
전사 기준으로도 수주목표 90.5%를 채워넣었다. 수주잔고 또한 상선 421억 
달러/전사 609억 달러로 각각 작년 말 대비 12.6%/13.2% 증가하였다. 해양부문과 
엔진기계부문은 조선사업부보다도 높은 이익률로 든든한 실적의 기반이 되고 있다.  
최근 HD현대그룹은 미국의 EPC강자 Kiewit과 미국 합작법인 설립에 관한 계약을 
맺고 그린필드 기반의 미국 신규 조선소 건설을 추진하고 있다. 빠르면 올해 중으로 
건립과 관련한 세부 사항이 공개될 예정이며 HD현대중공업이 중추적인 역할을 담당할 
것으로 보인다. 일찍부터 브라운필드 방식으로 접근한 동종사와 달리 다소 시간은 
걸렸지만 과감하게 결정된 투자다. 조선소 예상 준공 역시 2028년 이후로 예상되어 
당장 기업가치에 반영하기는 어려우나 엔진 증설과 함께 2028년 후에는 분명한 실적 
상향 요인이 될 것으로 예상한다. 우직하게 본업에 충실하며 신중하게 미래를 준비하는 
조선소, 귀감이다.

Document 262: AI周期能持续多久？

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI周期能持续多久？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-26
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

⾼盛Eric Sheridan明确AI周期三⼤终结信号：终端需求萎缩、资本断流、电⼒短缺。认为
超⼤规模云⼚商15%回报率可期，周期需五⾄⼋年兑现，市场⽤六个⽉考核周期丈量⻓期
基建，幻灭难免。Social Capital创始⼈Chamath则警告，新兴云⼚商以短期算⼒租赁收⼊
偿还⻓期债务，GPU利⽤率若⽆法突破75%将引发债务危机。
摘要：
2026年11⽉，美国中期选举尘埃落定，美股AI板块的剧烈震荡与信贷市场的信⽤利差⾛阔，正
在把华尔街拉⼊⼀场关于“AI投资周期持久性”的深度拷问。
11⽉25⽇，⾼盛最新⼀期播客中，主持⼈ Allison Nathan 邀请了⾝处算⼒基建与企业软件⼀线
的Social Capital创始⼈Chamath Palihapitiya，以及⾼盛⾸席美国互联⽹研究分析师Eric
Sheridan。
两位嘉宾围绕新兴云⼚商的商业漏洞、企业端迟迟未兑现的投⼊产出⽐（ROI），以及科技巨头
的财务兜底能⼒，完整拆解了本轮AI周期的真实账本与潜在拐点。
⾼盛分析师Eric Sheridan认为超⼤规模云⼚商回报率可轻松超过15%红线，但明确三⼤周期终

结信号：终端需求萎缩、资本断流、电⼒短缺。
Social Capital创始⼈Chamath Palihapitiya警告，新兴云⼚商本质是"反向银⾏"，以短期算⼒租
赁收⼊偿还⻓期债务，GPU利⽤率若⽆法突破75%将引发债务危机。
他还指出，当前AI计费模式停留在"按Token收费"的1999年阶段，企业ROI尚未兑现。
算⼒基建的“时间差陷阱”：电⼒瓶颈与新兴云的反向银⾏危机
每⼀轮科技基建周期都会遭遇“建设前置”与“变现滞后”的久期错配，但在本轮AI浪潮中，这种错
配被物理世界的供电瓶颈和激进的⾦融杠杆急剧放⼤。
过去⼀年，市场对算⼒扩张的预期建⽴在芯⽚出货量的线性推演上，却严重低估了数据中⼼从
拿地到通电的物理阻⼒。
与此同时，⼀批依托资产⽀持证券（ABS）⼤举举债购买GPU的新兴云⼚商，正在复制航空业
的⾼危商业模型：背负⻓达六到⼋年的重资产折旧与融资债务，却在现货市场上按⼩时出售极
易贬值的算⼒座位。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⼀旦英伟达按年度节奏推出新⼀代芯⽚，或者⼤模型推理效率⼤幅提升，这些缺乏底层硬资产
壁垒的“算⼒⼆房东”将⾯临严峻的利⽤率考验。近期部分AI相关信贷产品的利差⾛阔和信⽤违
约互换（CDS）飙升，正是敏锐的债券市场提前嗅到了这种结构性脆弱。
Chamath Palihapitiya 过去⼀年将投资重⼼从芯⽚转向了底层的“⼟地、电⼒与机房外壳”，⽬前
已在全美积累了6吉⽡的电⼒资产。他在访谈中⽤⼀线实操经历点破了基建交付的残酷现实：
谈及新兴云⼚商的财务脆弱性，Chamath 给出了⼀个极具穿透⼒的⾦融类⽐：
他进⼀步警告称，就像航空公司上座率必须达到80%⾄85%才能盈亏平衡⼀样，新兴云⼚商的
GPU利⽤率必须跨越70%⾄75%的⻔槛才能在顶部25%的区间赚钱：
停留在“1999年”的商业模式：Agent越忙，企业越亏？
资本开⽀能否持续，最终取决于终端企业能否从AI中赚到真⾦⽩银。然⽽，当前AI产业的商业化
正卡在⼀个尴尬的瓶颈上：技术使⽤量在飙升，企业财务报表上的利润却纹丝不动。
这种背离源于计费模式与组织惯性的双重掣肘。

在计费层⾯，主流⼤模型⼚商依然停留在按“百万Token”售卖算⼒库存的阶段，企业买到的是计
算量⽽⾮业务结果。
在组织层⾯，企业中层员⼯普遍担忧AI会取代⾃⼰的岗位，导致新技术在⼤型组织内部的渗透远
慢于实验室预期，即便局部岗位节省了⼯时，这些零散的时间也往往在冗⻓的业务流程中被⽩
⽩耗散，未能转化为公司整体的全要素⽣产率。
Chamath创办的企业级AI公司8090专⻔⽤于观察AI如何转化为实际回报，他⽤⾃家公司的真实
账本揭⽰了这⼀悖论：
要把这些电⼒资产真正通电运转，其中的复杂程度简直令⼈⿇⽊。德克萨斯州的矿产权纠纷、因
为地下有个化粪池⽽卡住的环境评估、尺⼨不合的混凝⼟基座，再加上燃⽓轮机供应商的严重积
压。把这些因素拼在⼀起，现实情况让我⾮常担忧。我看到的是市场对可⽤产能做出了海量承
诺，但实际交付量却很低，⽽能产⽣实际投资回报的商业⾯积更是少之⼜少。
新兴云⼚商本质上就是⼀家反向运转的银⾏。传统银⾏吸收的是储⼾随时可能提⾛的短期存款，
发放的是⼗年、⼗⼀年期的按揭和商业贷款，也就是‘短借⻓贷’；新兴云⼚商把这套逻辑彻底颠

倒了过来，他们的收⼊⾼度依赖客⼾按⼩时计费的短期投机性需求，但背负的债务却是⻓期且固
定的。
如果你只是⼀堆租赁合同的集合体，本质上你就是⼀个中间商。未来18个⽉，如果⼤企业不能
从实验阶段转向⼤规模真实消费，Token消耗量就⽆法爆发，利⽤率就会卡在75%到85%的灰⾊
地带，久期错配的债务问题迟早会反噬。
如果你把8090内部的智能体⼯作流画成图表，你会看到Token消耗量直线上升，Agent的循环调
⽤次数⾼得离谱。我公司⾥⼲活的Agent数量⼤概⽐⼈类员⼯多出⼀个数量级，⽽且还在涨。但
如果你问我，今天的投资回报率（ROI）⽐昨天⾼了1%吗？我的回答是⼤概没有。事实上，我

### Page 3

在他看来，2026年的AI⾏业像极了1999年的互联⽹泡沫前夜：
他同时指出，翻遍当下美股上市公司的财报，每股收益（EPS）的增⻓⼤多来⾃股票回购，利
润率的改善多源于通胀带来的定价权，真正敢公开归功于AI驱动的盈利增⻓公司“少之⼜少，⼏
乎不存在”。
15%回报率红线与周期的三⼤刹⻋⽚
⾯对资本市场对“巨额烧钱”的质疑，⾼盛⾸席美国互联⽹研究分析师Eric Sheridan从历史计算周
期的规律出发，给出了相对冷静的回应。
在华尔街看来，过去12到18个⽉AI板块经历的三次显著回调，本质上是投资者试图⽤“六个⽉的
财报考核周期”，去丈量需要“五到⼋年才能兑现”的底层计算基建。
虽然内存等硬件成本出现了超预期的通胀，企业端普及也存在员⼯抵触的摩擦，但超⼤规模云
⼚商⼿握的巨额积压订单正在提供可量化的回报可⻅度。
Eric Sheridan 明确指出，市场当前的焦虑完全可以理解，但并未脱离历史轨道：
针对投资者最关⼼的投⼊资本回报率，Eric 披露了⾼盛内部的测算逻辑与最新财报季的验证信
号：

的利润率反⽽下降了1%，因为更多的Agent在跑更多的任务，导致我的总⽀出增加了，哪怕单
个Token的成本正在下降。
1999年，雅虎、AOL这些互联⽹巨头的核⼼商业模式是按千次展⽰（CPM）卖⼴告；到了2026
年，AI巨头的核⼼商业模式是按百万Token收费，两者如出⼀辙。直到三四年后，⾕歌彻底改变
了游戏规则，从按展⽰收费转向按点击或按转化付费（CPA），这意味着客⼾从‘购买库存’转向了
‘购买结果’。
我们今天依然坐在2026年买着以百万量计的Token。谁能率先在规模化层⾯转向‘按结果付费’，
谁就能让像我这样的企业主拿到财务团队算得清的账单，⽐如说‘你这个⽉花了100万美元，但带
来了150万美元的产出，回报率极佳，继续投⼊’。但今天，我还做不到这⼀点。
我分析过的每⼀个计算周期都会经历这个过程。随着周期向纵深推进、投资规模不断滚⼤，市场
审查必然会变得越来越严厉。⼀旦资本投⼊的时间点与回报可⻅度之间出现久期错配，幻灭感就
会蔓延到整个周期中，我们现在正处在这个阶段。
云业务、移动计算、桌⾯计算通常都需要五到⼋年的周期，但⼤多数⼈总希望它们按六个⽉的节

奏兑现。投资者渴望像管理实时库存⼀样去调配前瞻性的算⼒资本，但这在现实中根本⾏不通
——你必须先买地、建数据中⼼、塞满计算设备并点亮通电，然后才能把算⼒卖给别⼈。
我们围绕⾏业最低15%的投⼊资本回报率做过测算，我们认为超⼤规模云⼚商⾮常容易达到15%
以上的回报。甚⾄不需要100%的积压订单转化为收⼊，也不需要在此基础上产⽣额外增⻓，就
能实现这⼀回报⽔平。

### Page 4

Eric 补充表⽰：
那么，究竟什么⼒量会真正终结本轮AI投资周期？Eric讲道：
Eric划出了三条决定AI投资周期⽣死的底线：
谁在为万亿豪赌兜底？
如果AI基建的回报兑现⽐预期更漫⻓，谁将最终承担资产负债表受损的代价？
两位嘉宾在⻛险定价上达成了⾼度⼀致：⻛险正沿着产业链条向外围扩散，拥有强⼤核⼼现⾦
流的科技巨头依然稳坐钓⻥台，⽽⾼度依赖外部融资和单⼀算⼒租赁的边缘玩家正在被市场重
新定价。
Chamath 通过AI梳理了所有上市云⼚商与新兴云⼚商的监管⽂件，将它们的产能建设承诺与“照
付不议（Take-or-pay）”债务进⾏了交叉⽐对。他发现，绝⼤多数新兴云⼚商的特殊⽬的载体
（SPV）背后，其实都站着提供投资级信⽤增级的科技巨头：
Chamath认为科技巨头最终是对的，但问题只在于“什么时候”。Chamath说：
Eric Sheridan则从⼆级市场估值的⻆度印证了这⼀⻛险分层：
在刚刚过去的财报季，微软和亚⻢逊都针对投⼊资本回报率给出了⾮常详尽的解答，两家公司的
股价都在财报后实现了双位数上涨；Alphabet 明确表⽰其云业务回报率远⾼于加权平均资本成

本；Meta 的营收增速更是保持在25%左右，⽽12个⽉前我曾预测他们本季度增速会降⾄12%，
正是AI在核⼼⼴告业务中的应⽤为⼴告主创造了实打实的收⼊增量。
⼤家总想让我从股价的⻆度回答这个问题，但周期放缓绝不会是⼀个由股价驱动的决定。超⼤规
模云⼚商承受股价阵痛的忍耐周期，远⽐我每天接触的绝⼤多数机构投资者要⻓。
真正可能阻断或推迟这⼀主题演进的只有三件事：终端需求、资本获取能⼒以及电⼒供应。
只要电⼒跟不上、资本断了流、或者需求塌了⽅——这三块拼图只要缺了任何⼀块，我们就会陷
⼊⼀种更永久性的停滞，⽽不仅仅是⼀次短暂的幻灭低⾕。
所有路径最终都指向了为这场基建狂潮提供资⾦源头的投资级巨头，也就是超⼤规模云⼚商。有
意思的是，这些⼤块头已经从过去⾃由现⾦流极其充沛、忙着回购股票、⼏乎不发债的状态，变
成了⾃由现⾦流⼤幅转负，开始发债甚⾄发⾏股票来为基建融资。他们押上了全部⾝家，坚信⾃
⼰能为这场建设兜底。
就像当年互联⽹泡沫⼀样，所有关于⽣产⼒和收⼊增⻓的预测最终都应验了，但⼤家全都在时间

点上犯了错——市场从2002年⼀路出清到2007年，紧接着⼜碰上全球⾦融危机，整个⾏业直到
2010、2011年才真正站稳脚跟。不过，超⼤规模云⼚商不会有事，他们拥有极其扎实的核⼼业
务和近乎完美的资产负债表。

Topics  Regulatory reform in India's insurance sector

Over the past quarter century, the Indian government has pursued the liberalization of the country's insurance market, 
with the aim of bringing stability to people's living standards and strengthening financial intermediation functions. 
Recently, however, it has begun to reverse course, tightening rules in order to better protect consumers.

■ India's insurance market has grown rapidly with deregulation 
The Indian government has long pursued 
the development of the country's insurance 
market, with the aim of bringing stability to 
people's living standards and strengthening 
financial intermediation functions. This 
stance can be attributed to two background 
factors:  
The first is that India's social security 
system remains underdeveloped. As a result, 
enrollment in health and life insurance can 
provide a vital safety net for mitigating the 
risk of sharp declines in household income 
due to unexpected illnesses or accidents.  
The second is that the sustainable 
development of the Indian economy requires 
the accumulation of long-term and stable 
domestic capital, separate from short-term 
bank deposits, in order to finance the 
development of infrastructure and the 
nurturing of industry. The hope has been that 
the enormous sums of money collected and 
managed by insurance companies can serve 
as a reliable source for this long-term 
financing.  
These factors have led the Indian 
government to walk a path of gradual 
deregulation over the past quarter century. 
The process began with the liberalization of 
market entry by private and foreign 
companies in 2000, which was followed by 
a lifting of the ban on "bancassurance" (the 
sale of insurance through bank branches) in 
2002, the raising of the ceiling on foreign 
ownership, and other reforms.  
This series of deregulatory measures 
spurred the entry of numerous private and foreign 
firms, causing market competition to intensify. Several 
Japanese insurers, including Nippon Life and the Tokio 
Marine Group, also entered the market, helping to drive 
investment in India.  
Besides deregulation related to market entry and 
sales, other factors also contributed to the rapid growth 
of India's insurance market, namely the expansion of 
the middle class, improvements in household financial 
literacy, and the digitalization of insurance services. 
Premium income grew at a pace exceeding economic 
growth, and in recent years insurance companies' assets 
under management have reached a level equivalent to 
Year Event
Establishment of the IRDAI (Insurance Regulatory
and Development Authority of India)

domestic private firms and foreign entities

3 
incentives to sell products and inappropriate sales practices. This should shore up the credibility of the 
insurance market and the foundation for its sustainable development. 
On the minus side, there are worries about side effects arising from the transition. As rules on selling 
become stricter, insurance companies and the financial institutions acting as insurance agents will inevitably 
see some temporary moderation in the pace of their earnings growth. Moreover, given that insurance 
companies and financial institutions are major investors in India's financial markets, tighter regulation is 
likely to exert downward pressure on equity and bond prices. 
These changes in the insurance market provide an important perspective for assessing the future 
trajectory of the Indian economy. They illustrate that rapid market expansion driven by liberalization can 
create a variety of problems. And this dynamic is not limited to the insurance sector, as it can also occur 
across many other industries. At the same time, the redesign and recalibration of regulations in response to 
such developments can provide a foundation for a new stage of growth. The new insurance regulations 
could constitute an early example of this phenomenon, so will be closely watched to see what changes they 
bring in the years ahead.  
  (Shotaro Kumagai)

Topics  Talent travel curbs may weigh on China’s growth

In September, the Chinese government introduced new exit-entry rules. Indicative of an effort to retain talent in order 
to prevent technology leakage, the rules could narrow opportunities for China to absorb overseas knowledge and weigh 
on its economic growth over the medium to long term.

■ Scope of overseas travel restrictions expanded 
On September 15, the Chinese 
government brought in new rules 
specifying conditions and screening 
procedures for entry and exit. The new 
rules explicitly state that the Ministry of 
Commerce or other departments may 
prohibit Chinese citizens from leaving the 
country if they violate export-control or 
technology import/export regulations and 
are deemed likely to threaten national 
industrial or technological security.  
Under China's Exit and Entry 
Administration Law, which came into 
effect in 2013, defendants and suspects in 
criminal cases, as well as persons involved 
in unsettled civil cases whom courts have 
prohibited from leaving the country, had 
already been subject to exit restrictions. In 
addition, Chinese citizens could be denied 
permission to leave the country if the 
competent departments determined that 
their departure might endanger national 
security or interests. Aside from formal exit 
bans, personnel who handle state secrets 
and officials in the public sector have long 
been subject to controls such as centralized 
passport management and the need to 
obtain approval in advance of overseas travel. What the new rules effectively do is make it clear that exit 
can also be restricted when there are issues from the standpoint of economic security. 
Against this backdrop, it was reported at the end of September that overseas travel restrictions already 
imposed on key personnel in the AI and semiconductor sectors had been expanded to include their spouses, 
children, and other family members. According to these reports, these key personnel include prominent AI 
startup founders, senior executives at major private enterprises, and core R&D talent. Although the measure 
does not constitute a blanket travel ban, affected individuals and their families are reportedly required to 
obtain government approval for exit even in the case of short private trips or business travel. 
This regulatory tightening is thought to be related to the technological rivalry between the U.S. and China. 
As competition surrounding critical technologies such as generative AI intensifies, the Chinese government 
appears to view not only technologies themselves, but also the researchers and engineers who create them, 
as strategic assets. By widening travel curbs to encompass family members, the authorities are likely 
seeking to prevent overseas emigration by individuals or the relocation of R&D operations abroad. A 
noteworthy aspect of the recent reports is that the controls apparently now extend beyond the public sector 
to key personnel at private enterprises, as well as their families. Restrictions like these on talent mobility 
could undermine the mechanisms of technology absorption and productivity improvement that have 
hitherto facilitated China's growth.

■ China's productivity gains have been supported by market opening and technology 
absorption 
Productivity exerts a substantial influence on 
economic growth. Total factor productivity (TFP), an 
indicator of an economy's overall production 
efficiency, provides an estimate of efficiency in 
production that cannot be explained solely in terms of 
inputs of capital and labor, and reflects factors such as 
levels of technology and efficiency of resource 
allocation. 
China's TFP has been rising for many years. In 
addition to the transition to a market economy and the 
growth of private enterprises since the country adopted 
the policy of reform and opening up, Chinese 
productivity has benefitted from the absorption of 
foreign technology and managerial know‑how through 
trade and direct investment. A labor shift from 
agriculture to manufacturing and other sectors, along 
with competition among firms and the weeding out of 
weaker players, has also helped deliver productivity 
gains across the economy.  
Nevertheless, China's TFP remains low relative to 
other major economies. According to estimates from 
Penn World Table, China's TFP in 2023 was less than 
half that of the U.S., and it was also below the levels not 
only of Germany and other major European economies 
and of South Korea and Japan, but of Thailand and 
Malaysia, too. Factors behind this include inefficient 
resource allocation and the slow diffusion of advanced 
technologies and management practices. Preferential 
financial and institutional treatment for state‑owned 
enterprises, regional protectionism, and mechanisms 
that impede market entry and exit have distorted the 
allocation of resources, preventing capital and labor 
from being fully utilized by more productive firms and sectors. Moreover, although China has achieved 
strong competitiveness in certain advanced industries, such as electric vehicles and semiconductors, the 
leading-edge technologies and management practices developed in these sectors have not sufficiently 
diffused to the service sector and small and medium‑sized enterprises, which form the foundation of the 
economy, and this has constrained overall productivity growth.  
■ Moves to contain technology could hinder growth over the medium to long term 
Efforts to retain talent in order to prevent technology leakage could further delay China's productivity 
catch‑up with advanced economies. While restrictions on overseas travel by elite professionals may help 
with the retention of domestic R&D talent and protection of current technological advantages, they could 
also reduce opportunities to absorb overseas knowledge and to jointly develop new technologies with 
foreign partners. The transfer of technology and know‑how does not occur solely through the sharing of 
"explicit knowledge," in the form of academic papers, technical blueprints, and so on. "Tacit knowledge," 
which is transmitted through face‑to‑face discussion, collaborative work, and the acquisition of experience 
in production settings, is also crucial. If travel controls delay or prevent participation in international 
conferences, joint research projects, and technology exchanges with overseas companies, opportunities to 
absorb such knowledge may be lost, slowing technological innovation and improvements in production 
processes. Furthermore, if obtaining travel approval becomes more time‑consuming or less predictable, the 
overseas assignment or international deployment of technical personnel could also be obstructed. 
The new rules also cover the entry of foreign nationals. If uncertainty surrounding the administration of 
entry and exit controls increases, it could create obstacles to attracting and retaining highly skilled talent 
from abroad. At a time when inefficient resource allocation and the slow diffusion of advanced knowledge 
are already weighing on productivity growth in China, curbs on people-to- people exchange could further

impede both the absorption and creation of knowledge, as well as its practical application. Although the 
magnitude of the impact will depend on the scope of application and actual implementation on the ground, 
restrictions on talent exchanges and overseas travel that reflect excessive emphasis on technology 
protection could slow China's TFP growth over the medium to long term and act as a headwind to the 
Chinese economy by lowering its potential growth rate.

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
ECONOMIC INSIGHT
As expected, the RBA Monetary Policy Board (MPB) lifted
the cash rate 25bps to 4.85% at its November meeting.
This follows the increase at the previous (late-September)
meeting and is a marked turnaround from the outlook back
in August. The post-meeting media statement cited higher

global energy prices resulting from the Middle East conflict,
tighter domestic capacity pressures and an increase in
inflation expectations for the decision.
However, the decision was a split vote, 6 votes to 3, a
possibility we have flagged for some time. In the post-meeting
media conference, Governor Bullock explained that the
dissenting minority put more weight on the downside risks and
did not see signs of wages growth picking up as much as the

staff forecasts implied. Those members were reportedly more
confident that slower growth would prevent higher inflation
expectations becoming entrenched.
Financial conditions are restrictive and this is expected to
bring inflation down over time. Because the RBA assesses the
risks to the inflation outlook to still be skewed to the upside,
the outlook for coming meetings will still be for a decision
between holding and hiking. But we note that the post-meeting

statement omitted September’s language “including increasing
the cash rate target further if needed” from the commitment to
do what is necessary to get inflation down.
In the post-meeting media conference, the Governor
commented that after five hikes, this is probably a good time
to “take stock”. Together with the dissents, we read this as
confirming our current view that the MPB will keep rates
on hold in December. There is also considerable data flow

between now and then, which could go either way, including
the Q3 national accounts.
Similarly, a lot could happen between now and February, but
we see it as the next most likely timing for a hike, should one
eventuate. We still see that as a risk rather than a base case.
Revised forecasts published in the accompanying Statement
on Monetary Policy (SMP) show underlying inflation lingering
around current rates for longer. This contrasts with the
previous SMP forecasts in August, which showed trimmed

mean inflation returning to the 2–3%yr target range by late
2027 and a little below the 2.5%yr target midpoint in 2028.
We note, however, that the conditioning assumptions on oil
prices, which are based on futures prices, were at a local peak
at the time the forecasts were finalised.
RBA hikes cash rate to new high of 4.85%, a December pause likely
Luci Ellis
Chief Economist, Westpac Group
The RBA’s GDP growth forecasts for 2027 and 2028 were
both revised down –0.3%pts to 1.3% for 2027 and 1.5% in

2028, despite an upward revision to the near term. This is
well below even the RBA’s downbeat view of potential output
growth. Within the GDP aggregate, recent misses and leading
indicators have required upward revisions to the near-term
outlooks for business and dwelling investment, and downside
revisions to public demand. These revisions were offset further
out with downward revisions to the 2028 outlook for business
and dwelling investment and an upward revision to public

demand. Household consumption forecasts were revised down
across most of the profile.
With the unemployment rate already at 4.7% in August and
September – a rate the RBA did not forecast until end-2027 in
its August SMP forecasts – the RBA has lifted its unemployment
forecast to show a peak of 5.1% in at end-2028, the end of the
forecast horizon. This largely reflects stronger labour supply,
with forecasts for employment growth essentially unchanged.

We have repeatedly flagged that cost-of-living pressures are
likely to keep people in the labour force and thus boost the
participation rate. The RBA had previously not factored this
adequately into its forecasts but acknowledged in the SMP
that this mechanism had kept participation higher than it had
expected at the time of the August SMP.
However, because inflation surprised to the upside recently,
this additional labour supply has had no bearing on the

RBA’s assessment of capacity pressures. The RBA assesses
the domestic economy to still be a little tight, and tighter
than previously judged. The RBA’s framework for assessing
the degree of spare capacity or tightness in the economy
necessarily translates an upside surprise on inflation (or unit
labour costs) into a conclusion that the economy must be
tighter than previously judged. Indeed, the RBA’s estimate
of the NAIRU (unemployment rate consistent with inflation

stable at target) has been revised up to 4.75%.
The RBA highlighted that elevated inflation expectations may
be contributing to higher realised inflation. Consumer inflation
expectations have started to lift again but remain below where
they peaked earlier this year. However, the RBA appears
“RBA lifts cash rate 25bps to 4.85% in split
vote, citing “broadly based” domestic inflation
pressures, Middle East conflict and AI boom."

The AI boom is heavily cited in the SMP and other post-

Demand for AI-related goods is also cited as a driver of both

is a very different view of the risks from AI than the RBNZ
expressed last week. For the RBA, it is all about inflation, a
little bit about pressure on domestic construction resources,
and nothing about either an ugly unwind of the current
financing boom or a future productivity dividend.

THE WEEK THAT WAS
Cliff Notes: whiplash
total to $11.1bn. This is another clear marker of the scale of
Australia’s data-centre investment boom, which should feature
prominently in both business investment and imports in the Q3
National Accounts.
Offshore, the world has been focused on the US mid-term

elections. In the House, Senate and a series of State Governor
races, the Democrats experienced resounding victory. Come
2027, the Democrats will have a comfortable majority in both
houses of Congress and are expected to make use of this
strength to respond to voter concerns, particularly over cost-
of-living pressures. The election result also limits President
Trump’s ability to fund an extended military action against
Iran. Notably, both immediately before and after the election,

President Trump and the administration have exhibited a greater
interest in furthering negotiations with Iran. It is therefore not
a surprise that Brent oil has backed off its most recent highs
around USD118 last Friday, although the scale of the drop to
near USD93 yesterday is arguably too optimistic. Brent has since
traded back up to above USD99, still 16% below Friday’s peak.
While the initial reaction to the US election result was a
robust broad-based rally in major equity indexes, concerns

over the potential for tighter regulation of AI and the related
infrastructure buildout jolted US equities lower overnight – the
Nasdaq falling 5.5% and S&P500 4.4%, both a multiple of
the prior session’s gains. That said, US term interest rates and
FOMC rate expectations have also been scaled back; so, while
tech may come under greater scrutiny, a foundation for better
outcomes across the rest of the US economy may become
evident in time. US data out this week continued to suggest

their economy is in robust health overall. And, if tensions in
the Middle East abate, the FOMC’s concerns over US inflation
should subside. Gaining enough comfort for the FOMC to not
hike in December is a high bar, but the probability of further
hikes in 2027 should continue to fall.
Turning then to the UK, the Bank of England raised Bank Rate
by 25bps to 4.00% at their November meeting as expected.
On the Bank's baseline expectation, UK inflation is forecast to

rise from 3.5%yr at September to 5.00% by mid-2027. Activity
data has also surprised to the upside in recent months, though
"there remains limited evidence of knock-on effects on other
prices and wages".
In Australia, the RBA delivered another 25bp rate increase
at its November meeting, taking the cash rate to 4.85%. The
move was widely expected, reflecting the Board’s continued
concern around inflation following the recent run higher in
global energy prices, firmer domestic capacity pressures and

signs that inflation expectations have edged up. Inflation
is now not expected to return to the mid-point of the
target band until mid-2028. This is despite weaker growth
expectations and the unemployment rate trending up to
5.1% by end-2028 on the RBA’s forecasts.
Importantly, though, the decision was not unanimous, with
three members preferring to remain on hold given downside
risks to activity and the restraining influence of slower growth.
Governor Bullock’s suggestion that it was time to “take stock”

after five rate increases also struck a less hawkish note,
while the statement dropped September’s explicit reference
to raising rates further “if needed”. In our view, February
represents the next plausible opportunity for another hike, but
we see this only as a risk.
Policy’s effect on housing is becoming increasingly visible.
Cotality home prices fell a further 1.2% in October to now
be 6.7% below March’s peak. The cumulative price decline
is approaching the scale of previous housing corrections but

appears to have regained momentum after September’s rate
increase. Dwelling approvals saw a 5.1% headline gain in
September, but this was driven by volatile unit approvals while
detached house approvals fell sharply, suggesting higher rates
and the established-market downturn are beginning to weigh
on the construction pipeline.
Official data on nominal household spending meanwhile
rose 0.6% in September and 2.1% for Q3; however, much of
the recent acceleration looks to be price-driven, particularly

for fuel. Real spending volumes expanded by a much more
modest (but still solid) 0.7% in Q3.
The Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel provides a granular look at
income, spending and saving flows. The latest update suggests
that, beneath the resilient headline story, households are
increasingly being squeezed, particularly those with low savings
buffers. With another rate increase now delivered, housing
wealth declining and sentiment weak, Q3 increasingly looks like

the high-water mark for growth in consumer spending.
Lastly for Australia, in September the goods trade surplus
widened sharply to $2.8bn, with non-monetary gold trade
yet again adding considerable noise month-to-month. The
most notable development was another exceptionally strong

In the press conference, Governor Bailey recognised the

was a decision that needs to be made in December, but rather
a risk to be judged as additional information is received. This
points to further tightening being more likely in early 2027
than before, and only if conditions / risks warrant.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
No deals are done
This week’s developments underscore our message that
a rate hike at the RBNZ’s December meeting is far from a
done deal. New Zealand’s labour market remains softer
than expected, and that will be noted by those on the

policy committee who question the extent of second-round
inflation pressures. The rout in the US mid-term elections
has also taken significant heat out of oil prices for now,
although it’s unclear whether that situation will hold through
to next month. Our view on the endpoint for this OCR cycle
remains unchanged, but there’s a growing risk that the RBNZ
could slow the pace of tightening in the new year.
The September quarter labour market surveys pointed to

ongoing softness in the jobs market. The unemployment
rate rose from 5.6% to 5.7%, continuing a four-year run
of increases, and reaching its highest level since 2015.
Employment was softer than expected, and wage growth
remains subdued despite increasing cost-of-living pressures.
The Household Labour Force Survey (HLFS) showed a surprise
0.1% fall in employment in the September quarter, after a
downwardly revised 0.3% increase in the June quarter. That

went against the Monthly Employment Indicator (MEI) measure
of filled jobs, which had suggested some upward momentum
emerging in the jobs market (and notably, the HLFS measure
had outpaced the MEI for the previous nine straight quarters).
The fall in HLFS employment was accompanied by a fall in
the labour force participation rate from 70.6% to 70.4%.
These co-movements essentially reversed the surprisingly
large increases that we saw in the June quarter, which we had

suggested at the time were more likely due to sampling error
in the survey rather than a genuine surge.
We generally recommend focusing on the unemployment rate,
which is less vulnerable to sampling errors – and the message
here is one of softness. Our best interpretation is that, to the
extent that jobs are growing, it’s not fast enough to absorb the
growth in the population (which itself is a fairly low bar, as net
migration remains at a low ebb).

Even with the economy returning to growth over the last
year or so, hiring activity has remained weak. This may be
an ongoing legacy of employment trends in previous years.
Businesses have told us that they felt burned by the severe
skill shortages that developed over 2021-23, and looked to
hold on to workers to the extent they could during the 2024-
25 downturn. The flipside of this is that they may now find
themselves overstaffed even as activity picks up again.

This shows up most strikingly in labour force transition rates.
The rise in unemployment over the last few years has not
been driven by an increase in job losses, but by accumulation
of people who, once out of work, are finding it increasingly
difficult to get back in again. That also extends to young
people trying to enter the workforce for the first time – youth
unemployment of around 25% is the highest in the history of
the survey going back to 1986.

The Labour Cost Index (LCI) rose by 0.5% in the September
quarter, in line with our expectations overall but a bit weaker
in the details – private sector wages were up by 0.5%, while
there was a surprisingly large 0.7% increase in public sector
wages. There was a notable jump in the number of employers
citing cost-of-living adjustments as a reason for increasing pay
rates – although the size of those increases varied a great deal,
with many substantially less than the headline inflation rate.

Potential RBNZ implications
The labour market data was unequivocally weaker than
expected by either us or the RBNZ. While likely not a
gamechanger for the cycle overall, it will add ammunition to the
arguments of the doves on the Monetary Policy Committee that
a pause in interest rate rises will make sense soon – perhaps in
December or early next year. It’s likely the case that interest rate
markets were over-pricing the odds of a hike at the 9 December

MPS, so the subsequent pullback in pricing makes sense.
The message from the RBNZ in the October meeting was to
be careful about pricing in "done deals", and that the outlook
Michael Gordon
Senior Economist

### Page 7

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
7
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
will be data-dependent. As we noted at the time, alongside
measures of inflation expectations, the key domestic data
to watch ahead of the RBNZ’s 9 December meeting will
be indicators of what is happening to excess capacity (the
output gap), as this will be a key determinant of the Bank’s

assessment of the outlook for inflation over the coming year.
The RBNZ looks at a broad suite of indicators of labour market
slack, although their research found that only four of them
seem to have predictive power: the unemployment rate,
the vacancy to unemployment ratio, the QSBO measure of
labour as the main limiting factor to growth, and the rate of
transition between jobs. The first three of these all pointed
to a softening in the September quarter. (The fourth one is

calculated from income tax data, so it’s not publicly available
and even the RBNZ receives it only with a lag.)
In the eyes of the dovish MPC members, the softness in
the labour market indicators will likely push in the direction
of having greater comfort with the medium-term inflation
outlook. The outcome of the election, with the possibility of a
return to a dual inflation/employment mandate, could increase
the prominence of these factors. This likely won’t be an explicit

factor in the RBNZ’s reasoning at its December review, but it
may be in the minds of individual MPC members.
That said, it’s important to remember that in the RBNZ’s
forecasting framework, the GDP figures are the main
determinant of excess capacity in the economy. The most
recent GDP data tells us that the output gap probably
narrowed a bit through the June quarter, mainly reflecting
historical revisions. In light of the latest labour market figures,

there may be some downside risk to the outlook for Q3 GDP –
but there are conflicting indicators there, so it’s not that clear.
At last month’s surprise on-hold OCR decision, Governor
Breman lamented that there was no protocol for the RBNZ
to be able to signal how it was reading the economic data –
in particular the softer than expected Q3 CPI print – ahead
of the policy meeting. In that light, it will be interesting to
see whether they use some already-scheduled speaking

engagements to signal what they make of the labour market
data. Top of mind is Chief Economist Conway's 10 November
speech to the UBS Australasia Conference (pointedly titled
“How is the NZ economy tracking”). Governor Breman is also
speaking to the Institute of Directors on 19 November.
Our view remains that the December OCR hike is more likely
than not. But there will be a clear debate to be had between
the MPC hawks and doves, and it may again come down to a

vote. We still expect a 4% peak in the OCR for this cycle, but
the time taken to get there could be more flexible. Takes along
the lines of "3% is the terminal rate" seem rather hasty on the
basis of a single data release.
Aside from how the Committee interprets the labour market
data, the key considerations in December will be: the RBNZ’s
surveys of inflation expectations by households and businesses;
whether the US mid-term elections lead to a sustained de-

escalation in the Middle East and a pullback in oil prices; and
whether developments in the AI space exacerbate some MPC
members’ concerns about the global economic environment.
And then of course there’s the result of the General Election,
with voting closing on Saturday evening. The actual formation
of a government is likely to take weeks of negotiation, so the
outcome may not be known by the 9 December MPS but at least
the range of possibilities should be clearer. We’ll publish our

initial thoughts on the election results early on Monday morning.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
NZ: Oct Retail Card Spending (%mth)
Nov 12, Last: –0.4, Westpac f/c: 1.4
We’re forecasting a 1.4% rise in retail spending in October.
While that would be a solid increase, it’s in large part due to
the 11% rise in petrol prices over the past month. Stripping out

fuel spending, we’re forecasting a 0.6% rise in core spending
categories. Core spending has been very choppy recently,
and the increase we’re assuming follows falls in the past few
months. A result in line with our expectations would leave the
trend in core spending relatively flat since the start of this year
with the rise in fuel prices weighing on spending in other areas.
AUS: Nov Westpac-MI Consumer Sentiment (index)
Nov 10, Last: 84.4
Consumer sentiment fell 4.7% to 80.4 in Oct, returning to the

deeply pessimistic levels seen earlier in the year. Responses
over the course of the survey week showed a very sharp
deterioration after the RBA rate hike.
The Nov survey is in the field over the week ended Nov 7.
Interest rates will again factor with the RBA raising the cash
rate by another 25bps to 4.85%, an 18yr high. Average pump
prices also pushed higher, the national average just under
$2.40/litre, while the housing market downturn has continued

to broaden and deepen. The US mid-term election results may
have some effects on sentiment locally as well.
AUS: Q3 Housing Finance Approvals (%qtr)
Nov 11 Last: –5.2, Westpac f/c: –10
Market f/c: –8.5, Range: –15 to –5
The total value of housing finance approvals fell The value of
new dwelling finance approvals excl. refinance fell 5.2%qtr,
the largest quarterly drop in three years. Annual growth eased
to 7.3%yr. The weakness was accounted for mainly by lower

lending volumes: loan numbers were down 4.0%qtr (–0.6%yr),
while average loan size also contributed negatively.
Q3 is expected to see an even bigger decline with the full
impact of interest rate rises and tax policy changes showing
through. The value of both loan enquiries and turnover fell by
around 10% in the quarter. We expect total finance approvals
to show a 10% drop as well, with investor loan approvals likely
to be down 16%qtr and owner occupier approvals down 6%qtr.

9
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
US: Oct CPI (%mth)
Nov 11, Headline, Last: 0.6, Market f/c: 0.3, Westpac f/c: 0.4
US consumer prices rose by 0.6% in September, although
half of the gain was due to energy inflation, with core prices
instead up 0.3%. Over the year, prices were reported to have

risen 3.7%yr overall and 2.6%yr excluding food and energy.
Constructively, ex food, energy and shelter, annual inflation
met the FOMC’s medium-term target of 2.0%yr.
Come October, energy price pressures are set to recede
given the pre-election run higher in oil prices came late in the
month. A softer print for shelter is also likely to moderate core
momentum. We see a 0.4% gain for the headline measure and
a 0.2% result for core inflation as most probable. Looking to

2027, disinflation is expected to continue at a measured pace
towards the medium-term target.

Document 315: Do Italian households have what it takes to finance Europe’s next ideas_ (2026-11-18)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Do Italian households have what it takes to finance Europe’s next ideas_ (2026-11-18).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-11-18
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

18 November 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
DO ITALIAN HOUSEHOLDS HAVE WHAT IT TAKES TO FINANCE EUROPE’S NEXT IDEAS?
Andrea d’Aquino
Between 2019 and 2025, US value added grew at twice the pace of Europe’s, driven largely by technology and labour productivity1,2. Yet Europe
does not lack saving to finance a similar development: over the past ten years, Euro area gross national saving exceeded domestic investment
by about €300 bn a year, equivalent to 15% of productive investment3, and in 2024-2025 gross capital outflows to the United States averaged

€785 billion a year4. The situation described therefore creates a “European paradox”, where household particularly save but do not take on so
much risk to foster innovation as in the US.
Safety versus risk: how Italian and European households differ
In Q2 2026, Euro area households’ gross financial wealth grew by 6.5% y/y to approximately €37 trillion, of which just over 30% is held in
currency and deposits (€11,165 billion), compared with about 10% in the United States. Italy has the lowest share of currency and deposits

among the major Euro area economies (see Chart 1). In Q2 2026, Italian households’ financial wealth grew by 6.3% y/y, in line with the
Euro area, reaching €6,609 billion, of which 24.2% is held in currency and deposits (about €1,598 billion), compared with 28.3% in France
(€2,151 billion), 32% in Spain (€1,168 billion) and 36% in Germany (€3,660 billion).
1 Fabio Panetta; Il risparmio nella società che cambia, Banca d’Italia; 30th October 2026.
2 Luisa Carpinelli et al.; The Great Divide: productivity dynamics in the United States and euro area after the pandemic; Questioni di Economia e Finanza n. 924, Banca d’Italia; April

2025.
3 Productive investments are gross fixed investments minus residential construction.
4 It includes the item ‘other investments’, mainly made up of deposits, loans, and trade receivables.
SOURCE: EUROSTAT, BNL ECONOMIC RESEARCH
In Italy the largest share of households’ total financial assets is invested in equity
and investment fund shares, less in currency and deposits

Chart of the Week / 18 November 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
Italian risk is tied up mostly in unlisted firms
Judging by this metric, European households’ risk appetite is lower than that of their US counterparts. Another metric to look at: the listed
shares. They account for 5% of Euro area gross financial wealth5 (3.1% in Italy, 3.7% in France, 6.6% in Spain, 7.2% in Germany). However,
once holdings in unlisted companies are included, the share of risk capital rises to 15% in the Euro area, 34% in the United States and

more than 20% in Italy.
A large part of the gap reflects the wider presence in Europe of unlisted firms, largely family-owned. In Italy in particular, unlisted shares
account for 17.1% of gross financial wealth, just over 7 pp above the Euro area average, compared with 12.4% in France, 7.7% in Spain and
2.6% in Germany (see Chart 2). Unlisted family firms are on average smaller and less productive than the system as a whole, especially
among medium-sized and large firms and in technology- and knowledge-intensive sectors6.

Italian households’ risk capital is therefore largely held in forms that are hard to diversify and in firms far from the technological frontier,
while the risk capital reaching the youngest firms (less than 12 years old) is scarce, even though these firms are very active in innovation:
innovative start-ups account for just over 2% of young firms but hold about 44% of that category’s patents. Similarly, venture-capital-
backed firms are barely 0.2% of young firms but account for around 12% of total patents of the youngest firms7.

Italian inheritances will largely pass on bricks
Investment choices are also linked to demographics: risk appetite tends to rise until adulthood and then declines with advancing age8.
According to Banca d’Italia9, in the coming years in Italy, those born between 1966 and 1975 will benefit from wealth transfers to a greater
extent than earlier cohorts: inheritances would account for around 40% of their wealth, compared with 30% for those born between 1951

and 1965. All else being equal, households expecting to receive an inheritance hold more financial assets. What will change hands is
mainly real assets, above all housing: in 2022 (latest available data), households whose reference person was born between 1941 and
1950 held on average around 62% of their total wealth in dwellings, 28% in financial assets and almost 10% in equity stakes in businesses.
As a result, once households receive the inheritance, their portfolios could become more tilted towards housing, which may lead to a

rebalancing towards financial assets.
The challenge for the Euro area in the coming years will be to press ahead with the creation of a single market for capital, in order to
provide greater investment opportunities and avert the “European savings paradox”.
andrea.aquino@bnpparibas.com
